========== FILE: src\data\zones.ts ==========


========== FILE: src\hooks\useVehicleSimulation.ts ==========
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { getVehicles } from "../services/api";
import { vehicles as baseVehicles } from "../data/vehicles";
import type { VehicleData } from "../data/vehicles";

/* =========================================================
   SIMULATED VEHICLE
========================================================= */

export interface SimulatedVehicle extends VehicleData {
  latitude: number;
  longitude: number;
  lastUpdated: string;
  routeIndex: number;

  /* Backend-compatible fields */
  vehicle_id?: string;
  plate_number?: string;
  vehicle_type?: string;
}

/* =========================================================
   HOOK RETURN TYPE
========================================================= */

interface UseVehicleSimulationReturn {
  vehicles: SimulatedVehicle[];

  selectedVehicleId: string | null;

  setSelectedVehicleId: (
    id: string | null,
  ) => void;

  selectVehicle: (
    vehicleId: string,
  ) => void;

  clearVehicleSelection: () => void;

  loading: boolean;

  error: string | null;

  refreshVehicles: () => Promise<void>;
}

/* =========================================================
   DEFAULT COORDINATES
========================================================= */

const DEFAULT_LATITUDE = 12.9718;
const DEFAULT_LONGITUDE = 77.5945;

/* =========================================================
   FALLBACK VEHICLES
========================================================= */

const createFallbackVehicles =
  (): SimulatedVehicle[] => {
    return baseVehicles.map(
      (vehicle, index) => {
        /*
         * Your vehicles.ts already stores:
         *
         * position: [latitude, longitude]
         *
         * Therefore use that first.
         */

        const latitude =
          Number(
            vehicle.position?.[0] ??
              DEFAULT_LATITUDE,
          );

        const longitude =
          Number(
            vehicle.position?.[1] ??
              DEFAULT_LONGITUDE,
          );

        return {
          ...vehicle,

          latitude,

          longitude,

          lastUpdated:
            "Just now",

          routeIndex: index,
        };
      },
    );
  };

/* =========================================================
   NORMALIZE BACKEND VEHICLE
========================================================= */

const normalizeVehicle = (
  vehicle: any,
  index: number,
): SimulatedVehicle => {
  /* -------------------------------------------------------
     VEHICLE ID
  ------------------------------------------------------- */

  const vehicleId =
    vehicle.vehicle_id ??
    vehicle.id ??
    `VH-${String(
      index + 1,
    ).padStart(3, "0")}`;

  /* -------------------------------------------------------
     PLATE
  ------------------------------------------------------- */

  const plateNumber =
    vehicle.plate_number ??
    vehicle.number_plate ??
    vehicle.plate ??
    `KA-01-XX-${String(
      index + 1,
    ).padStart(4, "0")}`;

  /* -------------------------------------------------------
     VEHICLE TYPE
  ------------------------------------------------------- */

  const vehicleType =
    vehicle.vehicle_type ??
    vehicle.type ??
    "car";

  /* -------------------------------------------------------
     MODEL
  ------------------------------------------------------- */

  const vehicleModel =
    vehicle.model ??
    vehicle.vehicle_name ??
    vehicle.name ??
    "Simulated Vehicle";

  /* -------------------------------------------------------
     ROAD
  ------------------------------------------------------- */

  const roadName =
    vehicle.road ??
    vehicle.road_name ??
    "Main Road";

  /* -------------------------------------------------------
     ZONE
  ------------------------------------------------------- */

  const zoneName =
    vehicle.zone ??
    vehicle.zone_name ??
    "Zone A";

  /* -------------------------------------------------------
     SPEED
  ------------------------------------------------------- */

  const speed =
    Number(
      vehicle.speed ?? 0,
    );

  /* -------------------------------------------------------
     DIRECTION
  ------------------------------------------------------- */

  const direction =
    vehicle.direction ??
    "North";

  /* -------------------------------------------------------
     STATUS
  ------------------------------------------------------- */

  const status =
    vehicle.status ===
    "Stopped"
      ? "Stopped"
      : "Moving";

  /* -------------------------------------------------------
     POSITION
  ------------------------------------------------------- */

  /*
   * Backend can provide:
   *
   * latitude
   * longitude
   *
   * OR:
   *
   * lat
   * lng
   *
   * OR:
   *
   * position: [lat, lng]
   */

  const latitude =
    Number(
      vehicle.latitude ??
        vehicle.lat ??
        vehicle.position?.[0] ??
        DEFAULT_LATITUDE,
    );

  const longitude =
    Number(
      vehicle.longitude ??
        vehicle.lng ??
        vehicle.position?.[1] ??
        DEFAULT_LONGITUDE,
    );

  /* -------------------------------------------------------
     LAST UPDATED
  ------------------------------------------------------- */

  const lastUpdated =
    vehicle.last_updated ??
    vehicle.lastUpdated ??
    "Just now";

  /* -------------------------------------------------------
     RETURN NORMALIZED VEHICLE
  ------------------------------------------------------- */

  return {
    ...vehicle,

    /* Frontend fields */

    id: vehicleId,

    plate: plateNumber,

    type: vehicleType,

    model: vehicleModel,

    road: roadName,

    zone: zoneName,

    speed,

    direction,

    status,

    latitude,

    longitude,

    /* Backend-compatible fields */

    vehicle_id:
      vehicleId,

    plate_number:
      plateNumber,

    vehicle_type:
      vehicleType,

    /* Simulation fields */

    lastUpdated,

    routeIndex:
      index,
  } as SimulatedVehicle;
};

/* =========================================================
   MAIN HOOK
========================================================= */

export const useVehicleSimulation =
  (): UseVehicleSimulationReturn => {
    /* =====================================================
       VEHICLE STATE
    ===================================================== */

    const [
      vehicles,
      setVehicles,
    ] =
      useState<
        SimulatedVehicle[]
      >(
        createFallbackVehicles(),
      );

    /* =====================================================
       SELECTED VEHICLE
    ===================================================== */

    const [
      selectedVehicleId,
      setSelectedVehicleId,
    ] =
      useState<
        string | null
      >(null);

    /* =====================================================
       LOADING
    ===================================================== */

    const [
      loading,
      setLoading,
    ] =
      useState<boolean>(
        true,
      );

    /* =====================================================
       ERROR
    ===================================================== */

    const [
      error,
      setError,
    ] =
      useState<
        string | null
      >(null);

    /* =====================================================
       SELECT VEHICLE
    ===================================================== */

    const selectVehicle =
      useCallback(
        (
          vehicleId: string,
        ) => {
          setSelectedVehicleId(
            vehicleId,
          );
        },
        [],
      );

    /* =====================================================
       CLEAR SELECTION
    ===================================================== */

    const clearVehicleSelection =
      useCallback(
        () => {
          setSelectedVehicleId(
            null,
          );
        },
        [],
      );

    /* =====================================================
       LOAD VEHICLES
    ===================================================== */

    const refreshVehicles =
      useCallback(
        async () => {
          try {
            setLoading(true);

            setError(null);

            const response =
              await getVehicles();

            const backendVehicles =
              response?.data ??
              [];

            /* ---------------------------------------------
               BACKEND DATA AVAILABLE
            --------------------------------------------- */

            if (
              Array.isArray(
                backendVehicles,
              ) &&
              backendVehicles.length >
                0
            ) {
              const normalizedVehicles =
                backendVehicles.map(
                  (
                    vehicle: any,
                    index: number,
                  ) =>
                    normalizeVehicle(
                      vehicle,
                      index,
                    ),
                );

              setVehicles(
                normalizedVehicles,
              );

              return;
            }

            /* ---------------------------------------------
               EMPTY BACKEND RESPONSE
            --------------------------------------------- */

            setVehicles(
              createFallbackVehicles(),
            );
          } catch (err) {
            console.error(
              "Failed to load vehicles from backend:",
              err,
            );

            setError(
              "Backend vehicle data unavailable. Using simulation data.",
            );

            setVehicles(
              createFallbackVehicles(),
            );
          } finally {
            setLoading(false);
          }
        },
        [],
      );

    /* =====================================================
       INITIAL LOAD
    ===================================================== */

    useEffect(
      () => {
        refreshVehicles();
      },
      [
        refreshVehicles,
      ],
    );

    /* =====================================================
       LIVE VEHICLE SIMULATION
    ===================================================== */

    useEffect(
      () => {
        const interval =
          window.setInterval(
            () => {
              setVehicles(
                (
                  currentVehicles,
                ) => {
                  if (
                    currentVehicles.length ===
                    0
                  ) {
                    return currentVehicles;
                  }

                  return currentVehicles.map(
                    (
                      vehicle,
                    ) => {
                      /* -----------------------------------
                         STOPPED VEHICLE
                      ----------------------------------- */

                      if (
                        vehicle.status
                          ?.toLowerCase() ===
                        "stopped"
                      ) {
                        return {
                          ...vehicle,

                          lastUpdated:
                            "Just now",
                        };
                      }

                      /* -----------------------------------
                         MOVEMENT
                      ----------------------------------- */

                      const movement =
                        0.00005;

                      let newLatitude =
                        Number(
                          vehicle.latitude,
                        );

                      let newLongitude =
                        Number(
                          vehicle.longitude,
                        );

                      /* -----------------------------------
                         DIRECTION
                      ----------------------------------- */

                      switch (
                        vehicle.direction?.toLowerCase()
                      ) {
                        case "north":
                          newLatitude +=
                            movement;
                          break;

                        case "south":
                          newLatitude -=
                            movement;
                          break;

                        case "east":
                          newLongitude +=
                            movement;
                          break;

                        case "west":
                          newLongitude -=
                            movement;
                          break;

                        /*
                         * For diagonal directions
                         */

                        case "north-east":
                        case "northeast":
                          newLatitude +=
                            movement;
                          newLongitude +=
                            movement;
                          break;

                        case "north-west":
                        case "northwest":
                          newLatitude +=
                            movement;
                          newLongitude -=
                            movement;
                          break;

                        case "south-east":
                        case "southeast":
                          newLatitude -=
                            movement;
                          newLongitude +=
                            movement;
                          break;

                        case "south-west":
                        case "southwest":
                          newLatitude -=
                            movement;
                          newLongitude -=
                            movement;
                          break;

                        default:
                          newLongitude +=
                            movement;
                          break;
                      }

                      /* -----------------------------------
                         KEEP SIMULATION WITHIN
                         REASONABLE DIGITAL-TWIN AREA
                      ----------------------------------- */

                      if (
                        newLatitude >
                        13.1
                      ) {
                        newLatitude =
                          12.9718;
                      }

                      if (
                        newLatitude <
                        12.85
                      ) {
                        newLatitude =
                          12.9718;
                      }

                      if (
                        newLongitude >
                        77.75
                      ) {
                        newLongitude =
                          77.5945;
                      }

                      if (
                        newLongitude <
                        77.45
                      ) {
                        newLongitude =
                          77.5945;
                      }

                      /* -----------------------------------
                         UPDATED VEHICLE
                      ----------------------------------- */

                      return {
                        ...vehicle,

                        latitude:
                          Number(
                            newLatitude.toFixed(
                              7,
                            ),
                          ),

                        longitude:
                          Number(
                            newLongitude.toFixed(
                              7,
                            ),
                          ),

                        /*
                         * Keep position synchronized
                         * with latitude/longitude.
                         */

                        position: [
                          Number(
                            newLatitude.toFixed(
                              7,
                            ),
                          ),
                          Number(
                            newLongitude.toFixed(
                              7,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],

                        lastUpdated:
                          "Just now",

                        routeIndex:
                          vehicle.routeIndex +
                          1,
                      };
                    },
                  );
                },
              );
            },
            3000,
          );

        return () => {
          window.clearInterval(
            interval,
          );
        };
      },
      [],
    );

    /* =====================================================
       CLEAR INVALID SELECTION
    ===================================================== */

    useEffect(
      () => {
        if (
          selectedVehicleId &&
          !vehicles.some(
            (
              vehicle,
            ) =>
              vehicle.id.toLowerCase() ===
              selectedVehicleId.toLowerCase(),
          )
        ) {
          setSelectedVehicleId(
            null,
          );
        }
      },
      [
        vehicles,
        selectedVehicleId,
      ],
    );

    /* =====================================================
       RETURN
    ===================================================== */

    return {
      vehicles,

      selectedVehicleId,

      setSelectedVehicleId,

      selectVehicle,

      clearVehicleSelection,

      loading,

      error,

      refreshVehicles,
    };
  };

========== FILE: src\pages\Analytics.tsx ==========
import {
  Activity,
  BarChart3,
  CarFront,
  CircleGauge,
  MapPin,
  Radio,
  TrendingUp,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useMemo } from "react";

import { useVehicleSimulation } from "../hooks/useVehicleSimulation";

export default function Analytics() {
  const { vehicles } = useVehicleSimulation();

  const movingVehicles = vehicles.filter(
    (vehicle) => vehicle.status === "Moving",
  ).length;

  const stoppedVehicles = vehicles.filter(
    (vehicle) => vehicle.status === "Stopped",
  ).length;

  const averageSpeed = useMemo(() => {
    if (vehicles.length === 0) {
      return 0;
    }

    return Math.round(
      vehicles.reduce(
        (total, vehicle) => total + vehicle.speed,
        0,
      ) / vehicles.length,
    );
  }, [vehicles]);

  const fastestVehicle = useMemo(() => {
    if (vehicles.length === 0) {
      return null;
    }

    return vehicles.reduce((fastest, vehicle) =>
      vehicle.speed > fastest.speed
        ? vehicle
        : fastest,
    );
  }, [vehicles]);

  const slowestVehicle = useMemo(() => {
    if (vehicles.length === 0) {
      return null;
    }

    return vehicles.reduce((slowest, vehicle) =>
      vehicle.speed < slowest.speed
        ? vehicle
        : slowest,
    );
  }, [vehicles]);

  const zoneData = useMemo(() => {
    const data: Record<
      string,
      {
        total: number;
        moving: number;
        stopped: number;
        speed: number;
      }
    > = {};

    vehicles.forEach((vehicle) => {
      if (!data[vehicle.zone]) {
        data[vehicle.zone] = {
          total: 0,
          moving: 0,
          stopped: 0,
          speed: 0,
        };
      }

      data[vehicle.zone].total += 1;
      data[vehicle.zone].speed += vehicle.speed;

      if (vehicle.status === "Moving") {
        data[vehicle.zone].moving += 1;
      } else {
        data[vehicle.zone].stopped += 1;
      }
    });

    Object.keys(data).forEach((zone) => {
      if (data[zone].total > 0) {
        data[zone].speed = Math.round(
          data[zone].speed /
            data[zone].total,
        );
      }
    });

    return data;
  }, [vehicles]);

  const vehicleTypeData = useMemo(() => {
    const data: Record<string, number> = {};

    vehicles.forEach((vehicle) => {
      data[vehicle.type] =
        (data[vehicle.type] || 0) + 1;
    });

    return data;
  }, [vehicles]);

  const trafficScore = useMemo(() => {
    if (vehicles.length === 0) {
      return 0;
    }

    const movingPercentage =
      (movingVehicles / vehicles.length) * 100;

    return Math.round(
      Math.max(
        0,
        Math.min(100, movingPercentage),
      ),
    );
  }, [
    movingVehicles,
    vehicles.length,
  ]);

  return (
    <div className="metropolis-page">
      {/* =================================================
          HEADER
      ================================================= */}

      <div className="page-header">
        <div>
          <h1 className="page-title">
            Analytics
          </h1>

          <p className="page-subtitle">
            Smart-city traffic analytics and
            simulation intelligence
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="live-pill">
          <span className="live-dot" />
          ANALYTICS LIVE
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* =================================================
          KPI CARDS
      ================================================= */}

      <div className="grid-4">
        <AnalyticsCard
          icon={<CarFront size={21} />}
          label="Tracked Vehicles"
          value={vehicles.length.toString()}
          description="Vehicles in simulation"
        />

        <AnalyticsCard
          icon={<TrendingUp size={21} />}
          label="Moving Rate"
          value={`${trafficScore}%`}
          description="Vehicles currently moving"
        />

        <AnalyticsCard
          icon={<CircleGauge size={21} />}
          label="Average Speed"
          value={`${averageSpeed} km/h`}
          description="Fleet-wide average"
        />

        <AnalyticsCard
          icon={<MapPin size={21} />}
          label="Active Zones"
          value={Object.keys(
            zoneData,
          ).length.toString()}
          description="Zones with traffic"
        />
      </div>

      {/* =================================================
          TRAFFIC OVERVIEW
      ================================================= */}

      <div
        className="grid-2"
        style={{
          marginTop: 18,
          alignItems: "start",
        }}
      >
        {/* TRAFFIC PERFORMANCE */}

        <section className="panel">
          <div className="panel-header">
            <div>
              <h3 className="panel-title">
                Traffic Performance
              </h3>

              <p
                className="muted"
                style={{
                  marginTop: 5,
                  fontSize: 12,
                }}
              >
                Current simulation performance
              </p>
            </div>

            <BarChart3 size={18} />
          </div>

          <div style={{ padding: 18 }}>
            <AnalyticsMetric
              label="Moving Vehicles"
              value={movingVehicles}
              total={vehicles.length}
              suffix="vehicles"
              percentage={
                vehicles.length > 0
                  ? Math.round(
                      (movingVehicles /
                        vehicles.length) *
                        100,
                    )
                  : 0
              }
            />

            <AnalyticsMetric
              label="Stopped Vehicles"
              value={stoppedVehicles}
              total={vehicles.length}
              suffix="vehicles"
              percentage={
                vehicles.length > 0
                  ? Math.round(
                      (stoppedVehicles /
                        vehicles.length) *
                        100,
                    )
                  : 0
              }
            />

            <AnalyticsMetric
              label="Average Speed"
              value={averageSpeed}
              total={60}
              suffix="km/h"
              percentage={Math.min(
                100,
                Math.round(
                  (averageSpeed / 60) *
                    100,
                ),
              )}
            />
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* VEHICLE TYPES */}

        <section className="panel">
          <div className="panel-header">
            <div>
              <h3 className="panel-title">
                Vehicle Distribution
              </h3>

              <p
                className="muted"
                style={{
                  marginTop: 5,
                  fontSize: 12,
                }}
              >
                Vehicle types in simulation
              </p>
            </div>

            <CarFront size={18} />
          </div>

          <div style={{ padding: 18 }}>
            {Object.keys(
              vehicleTypeData,
            ).length === 0 ? (
              <div
                className="muted"
                style={{
                  textAlign: "center",
                  padding: 30,
                  fontSize: 11,
                }}
              >
                No vehicle data available.
              </div>
            ) : (
              Object.entries(
                vehicleTypeData,
              ).map(
                ([type, count]) => {
                  const percentage =
                    vehicles.length > 0
                      ? Math.round(
                          (count /
                            vehicles.length) *
                            100,
                        )
                      : 0;

                  return (
                    <div
                      key={type}
                      style={{
                        marginBottom: 18,
                      }}
                    >
                      <div
                        style={{
                          display: "flex",
                          justifyContent:
                            "space-between",
                          marginBottom: 7,
                        }}
                      >
                        <span
                          style={{
                            fontSize: 11,
                            fontWeight: 600,
                          }}
                        >
                          {type}
                        </span>

                        <span
                          className="muted"
                          style={{
                            fontSize: 10,
                          }}
                        >
                          {count} ·{" "}
                          {percentage}%
                        </span>
                      </div>

                      <div
                        style={{
                          height: 7,
                          borderRadius: 20,
                          background:
                            "rgba(100,160,190,.10)",
                          overflow: "hidden",
                        }}
                      >
                        <div
                          style={{
                            width: `${percentage}%`,
                            height: "100%",
                            borderRadius: 20,
                            background:
                              "#31d29a",
                          }}
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  );
                },
              )
            )}
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>

      {/* =================================================
          ZONE ANALYTICS
      ================================================= */}

      <section
        className="panel"
        style={{
          marginTop: 18,
        }}
      >
        <div className="panel-header">
          <div>
            <h3 className="panel-title">
              Zone Analytics
            </h3>

            <p
              className="muted"
              style={{
                marginTop: 5,
                fontSize: 12,
              }}
            >
              Traffic performance across city zones
            </p>
          </div>

          <MapPin size={18} />
        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            overflowX: "auto",
          }}
        >
          <table
            style={{
              width: "100%",
              minWidth: 720,
              borderCollapse:
                "collapse",
            }}
          >
            <thead>
              <tr
                style={{
                  color: "#688493",
                  fontSize: 10,
                  textTransform:
                    "uppercase",
                  letterSpacing: ".06em",
                }}
              >
                <th style={tableHeader}>
                  Zone
                </th>

                <th style={tableHeader}>
                  Vehicles
                </th>

                <th style={tableHeader}>
                  Moving
                </th>

                <th style={tableHeader}>
                  Stopped
                </th>

                <th style={tableHeader}>
                  Avg Speed
                </th>

                <th style={tableHeader}>
                  Condition
                </th>
              </tr>
            </thead>

            <tbody>
              {Object.entries(
                zoneData,
              ).map(
                ([zone, data]) => {
                  const condition =
                    data.stopped >
                    data.moving
                      ? "High"
                      : data.stopped > 0
                        ? "Moderate"
                        : "Low";

                  return (
                    <tr key={zone}>
                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        <strong>
                          {zone}
                        </strong>
                      </td>

                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        {data.total}
                      </td>

                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        {data.moving}
                      </td>

                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        {data.stopped}
                      </td>

                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        {data.speed}{" "}
                        km/h
                      </td>

                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        <span
                          className={
                            condition ===
                            "High"
                              ? "status status-high"
                              : condition ===
                                  "Moderate"
                                ? "status status-medium"
                                : "status status-low"
                          }
                        >
                          {condition}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                },
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* =================================================
          VEHICLE PERFORMANCE
      ================================================= */}

      <div
        className="grid-2"
        style={{
          marginTop: 18,
          alignItems: "start",
        }}
      >
        {/* FASTEST */}

        <section className="panel">
          <div className="panel-header">
            <div>
              <h3 className="panel-title">
                Fastest Vehicle
              </h3>

              <p
                className="muted"
                style={{
                  marginTop: 5,
                  fontSize: 12,
                }}
              >
                Highest current simulated speed
              </p>
            </div>

            <TrendingUp size={18} />
          </div>

          <div style={{ padding: 20 }}>
            {fastestVehicle ? (
              <VehicleHighlight
                vehicleId={
                  fastestVehicle.id
                }
                road={
                  fastestVehicle.road
                }
                zone={
                  fastestVehicle.zone
                }
                speed={
                  fastestVehicle.speed
                }
                status={
                  fastestVehicle.status
                }
              />
            ) : (
              <EmptyState />
            )}
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* SLOWEST */}

        <section className="panel">
          <div className="panel-header">
            <div>
              <h3 className="panel-title">
                Slowest Vehicle
              </h3>

              <p
                className="muted"
                style={{
                  marginTop: 5,
                  fontSize: 12,
                }}
              >
                Lowest current simulated speed
              </p>
            </div>

            <Activity size={18} />
          </div>

          <div style={{ padding: 20 }}>
            {slowestVehicle ? (
              <VehicleHighlight
                vehicleId={
                  slowestVehicle.id
                }
                road={
                  slowestVehicle.road
                }
                zone={
                  slowestVehicle.zone
                }
                speed={
                  slowestVehicle.speed
                }
                status={
                  slowestVehicle.status
                }
              />
            ) : (
              <EmptyState />
            )}
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>

      {/* =================================================
          SYSTEM STATUS
      ================================================= */}

      <section
        className="panel"
        style={{
          marginTop: 18,
        }}
      >
        <div className="panel-header">
          <div>
            <h3 className="panel-title">
              Analytics Engine Status
            </h3>

            <p
              className="muted"
              style={{
                marginTop: 5,
                fontSize: 12,
              }}
            >
              METROPOLIS intelligence services
            </p>
          </div>

          <Radio size={18} />
        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            padding: 18,
            display: "grid",
            gridTemplateColumns:
              "repeat(3, 1fr)",
            gap: 12,
          }}
        >
          <SystemStatus
            title="Vehicle Data"
            value="Connected"
          />

          <SystemStatus
            title="Traffic Analysis"
            value="Running"
          />

          <SystemStatus
            title="Analytics Engine"
            value="Operational"
          />
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* FOOTER */}

      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          gap: 8,
          marginTop: 14,
          paddingBottom: 20,
          color: "#64808d",
          fontSize: 10,
        }}
      >
        <Activity size={13} />

        Analytics are generated from the live
        METROPOLIS vehicle simulation engine.
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   ANALYTICS CARD
========================================================= */

function AnalyticsCard({
  icon,
  label,
  value,
  description,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  label: string;
  value: string;
  description: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="panel kpi-card">
      {icon}

      <div
        className="kpi-label"
        style={{
          marginTop: 14,
        }}
      >
        {label}
      </div>

      <div className="kpi-value">
        {value}
      </div>

      <div className="kpi-change">
        {description}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   ANALYTICS METRIC
========================================================= */

function AnalyticsMetric({
  label,
  value,
  total,
  suffix,
  percentage,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: number;
  total: number;
  suffix: string;
  percentage: number;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        marginBottom: 20,
      }}
    >
      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          justifyContent:
            "space-between",
          marginBottom: 7,
        }}
      >
        <span
          style={{
            fontSize: 11,
            fontWeight: 600,
          }}
        >
          {label}
        </span>

        <span
          className="muted"
          style={{
            fontSize: 10,
          }}
        >
          {value} {suffix}
          {total > 0 &&
            label !== "Average Speed" &&
            ` / ${total}`}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          height: 7,
          borderRadius: 20,
          background:
            "rgba(100,160,190,.10)",
          overflow: "hidden",
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            width: `${percentage}%`,
            height: "100%",
            borderRadius: 20,
            background:
              "#31d29a",
            transition:
              "width .3s ease",
          }}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   VEHICLE HIGHLIGHT
========================================================= */

function VehicleHighlight({
  vehicleId,
  road,
  zone,
  speed,
  status,
}: {
  vehicleId: string;
  road: string;
  zone: string;
  speed: number;
  status: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: "grid",
        gridTemplateColumns:
          "auto 1fr auto",
        gap: 15,
        alignItems: "center",
      }}
    >
      <div
        style={{
          width: 46,
          height: 46,
          borderRadius: 9,
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          justifyContent: "center",
          background:
            "rgba(20,190,235,.06)",
        }}
      >
        <CarFront size={21} />
      </div>

      <div>
        <strong
          style={{
            fontSize: 13,
          }}
        >
          {vehicleId}
        </strong>

        <div
          className="muted"
          style={{
            marginTop: 5,
            fontSize: 10,
          }}
        >
          {road} · {zone}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          textAlign: "right",
        }}
      >
        <strong
          style={{
            fontSize: 18,
          }}
        >
          {speed}
        </strong>

        <div
          className="muted"
          style={{
            fontSize: 9,
          }}
        >
          km/h
        </div>

        <span
          className={
            status === "Moving"
              ? "status status-low"
              : "status status-medium"
          }
        >
          {status}
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   SYSTEM STATUS
========================================================= */

function SystemStatus({
  title,
  value,
}: {
  title: string;
  value: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        padding: 15,
        border:
          "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.08)",
        borderRadius: 9,
        background:
          "rgba(100,160,190,.025)",
      }}
    >
      <div
        className="muted"
        style={{
          fontSize: 10,
          marginBottom: 8,
        }}
      >
        {title}
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          gap: 7,
          fontSize: 11,
          fontWeight: 600,
        }}
      >
        <span
          className="live-dot"
          style={{
            width: 6,
            height: 6,
          }}
        />

        {value}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   EMPTY STATE
========================================================= */

function EmptyState() {
  return (
    <div
      className="muted"
      style={{
        textAlign: "center",
        padding: 25,
        fontSize: 11,
      }}
    >
      No vehicle data available.
    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   TABLE STYLES
========================================================= */

const tableHeader: React.CSSProperties = {
  textAlign: "left",
  padding: "13px 16px",
  borderBottom:
    "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.08)",
};

const tableCell: React.CSSProperties = {
  padding: "14px 16px",
  borderBottom:
    "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.06)",
  fontSize: 11,
};


========== FILE: src\pages\Dashboard.tsx ==========
import {
  Activity,
  AlertTriangle,
  ArrowUpRight,
  CarFront,
  CircleGauge,
  Clock3,
  Gauge,
  MapPin,
  Radio,
  Route,
  ShieldCheck,
  TrendingUp,
  Zap,
} from "lucide-react";

import {
  useMemo,
} from "react";

import {
  useVehicleSimulation,
} from "../hooks/useVehicleSimulation";

export default function Dashboard() {
  const {
    vehicles,
    loading,
    error,
  } = useVehicleSimulation();

  const totalVehicles =
    vehicles.length;

  const movingVehicles =
    vehicles.filter(
      (vehicle) =>
        vehicle.status ===
        "Moving",
    ).length;

  const stoppedVehicles =
    vehicles.filter(
      (vehicle) =>
        vehicle.status ===
        "Stopped",
    ).length;

  const averageSpeed =
    useMemo(() => {
      if (!vehicles.length) {
        return 0;
      }

      return Math.round(
        vehicles.reduce(
          (total, vehicle) =>
            total +
            Number(
              vehicle.speed || 0,
            ),
          0,
        ) /
          vehicles.length,
      );
    }, [vehicles]);

  const movementRate =
    totalVehicles > 0
      ? Math.round(
          (movingVehicles /
            totalVehicles) *
            100,
        )
      : 0;

  const zoneTraffic =
    useMemo(() => {
      const zones = [
        "Zone A",
        "Zone B",
        "Zone C",
      ];

      return zones.map(
        (zone) => {
          const list =
            vehicles.filter(
              (vehicle) =>
                vehicle.zone ===
                zone,
            );

          const stopped =
            list.filter(
              (vehicle) =>
                vehicle.status ===
                "Stopped",
            ).length;

          const density =
            totalVehicles > 0
              ? Math.round(
                  (list.length /
                    totalVehicles) *
                    100,
                )
              : 0;

          const congestion =
            list.length > 0
              ? Math.min(
                  100,
                  Math.round(
                    density * 0.65 +
                      (stopped /
                        list.length) *
                        35,
                  ),
                )
              : 0;

          return {
            zone,
            vehicles:
              list.length,
            congestion,
          };
        },
      );
    }, [
      vehicles,
      totalVehicles,
    ]);

  const incidents =
    Math.max(
      2,
      Math.min(
        5,
        stoppedVehicles + 1,
      ),
    );

  const attention =
    Math.min(
      3,
      stoppedVehicles,
    );

  const systemState =
    loading
      ? "INITIALIZING"
      : error
        ? "SIMULATION"
        : "OPERATIONAL";

  return (
    <div className="metropolis-page dashboard-page">

      {/* ===================================================
          HERO
      =================================================== */}

      <section className="dashboard-hero">

        <div className="dashboard-hero-grid" />

        <div className="dashboard-hero-content">

          <div className="dashboard-eyebrow">

            <span className="eyebrow-line" />

            <span>
              METROPOLIS / COMMAND CENTER
            </span>

            <span className="eyebrow-version">
              v2.4
            </span>

          </div>

          <h1 className="dashboard-title">
            City Intelligence
            <span>
              Dashboard
            </span>
          </h1>

          <p className="dashboard-subtitle">
            Monitor the simulated city,
            analyze mobility patterns,
            detect operational anomalies,
            and control the digital twin
            in real time.
          </p>

          <div className="dashboard-hero-actions">

            <div className="hero-live-state">

              <span className="hero-live-dot" />

              <div>
                <strong>
                  SYSTEM LIVE
                </strong>

                <span>
                  Digital twin engine active
                </span>
              </div>

            </div>

            <div className="hero-clock">

              <Clock3 size={14} />

              <span>
                REAL-TIME MONITORING
              </span>

            </div>

          </div>

        </div>

        <div className="dashboard-hero-metrics">

          <HeroMetric
            label="CITY LOAD"
            value="63%"
            trend="+4.8%"
          />

          <HeroMetric
            label="NETWORK HEALTH"
            value="94%"
            trend="+2.1%"
          />

          <HeroMetric
            label="DATA STREAM"
            value="LIVE"
            trend="24 / 24"
          />

        </div>

      </section>

      {/* ===================================================
          KPI GRID
      =================================================== */}

      <section className="dashboard-kpi-grid">

        <MetricCard
          icon={
            <CarFront size={19} />
          }
          label="Active Vehicles"
          value={String(
            totalVehicles,
          )}
          detail="Vehicles inside simulation"
          accent="cyan"
          trend="+12.4%"
        />

        <MetricCard
          icon={
            <Radio size={19} />
          }
          label="Moving Fleet"
          value={`${movementRate}%`}
          detail={`${movingVehicles} vehicles moving`}
          accent="green"
          trend="+6.8%"
        />

        <MetricCard
          icon={
            <Gauge size={19} />
          }
          label="Average Speed"
          value={`${averageSpeed}`}
          suffix="km/h"
          detail="Across simulated fleet"
          accent="blue"
          trend="+3.2%"
        />

        <MetricCard
          icon={
            <AlertTriangle size={19} />
          }
          label="Active Incidents"
          value={String(
            incidents,
          )}
          detail={`${attention} requiring attention`}
          accent="amber"
          trend="MONITOR"
        />

      </section>

      {/* ===================================================
          OPERATIONS GRID
      =================================================== */}

      <section className="dashboard-command-grid">

        {/* SYSTEM MONITOR */}

        <div className="dashboard-panel system-panel">

          <PanelHeading
            eyebrow="SYSTEM MONITOR"
            title="City Operations"
            description="Current digital-twin infrastructure state"
            icon={
              <Activity size={18} />
            }
          />

          <div className="operations-list">

            <Operation
              icon={
                <ShieldCheck
                  size={16}
                />
              }
              title="Core System"
              detail="Simulation services"
              value={
                systemState
              }
              good={
                !loading &&
                !error
              }
            />

            <Operation
              icon={
                <CarFront
                  size={16}
                />
              }
              title="Vehicle Engine"
              detail="Fleet movement processor"
              value={
                totalVehicles
                  ? "RUNNING"
                  : "WAITING"
              }
              good={
                totalVehicles >
                0
              }
            />

            <Operation
              icon={
                <Radio size={16} />
              }
              title="Data Stream"
              detail="Live simulation feed"
              value={
                error
                  ? "SIMULATION"
                  : "CONNECTED"
              }
              good={!error}
            />

            <Operation
              icon={
                <MapPin size={16} />
              }
              title="Digital Twin"
              detail="City spatial model"
              value="ACTIVE"
              good
            />

          </div>

          <div className="system-health">

            <div className="health-header">

              <span>
                SYSTEM HEALTH
              </span>

              <strong>
                94%
              </strong>

            </div>

            <div className="health-track">

              <div
                className="health-progress"
                style={{
                  width: "94%",
                }}
              />

            </div>

            <div className="health-meta">

              <span>
                CPU 38%
              </span>

              <span>
                MEMORY 52%
              </span>

              <span>
                STREAM 98%
              </span>

            </div>

          </div>

        </div>

        {/* TRAFFIC INTELLIGENCE */}

        <div className="dashboard-panel traffic-panel">

          <PanelHeading
            eyebrow="TRAFFIC ANALYTICS"
            title="Mobility Intelligence"
            description="Vehicle density across city zones"
            icon={
              <TrendingUp size={18} />
            }
          />

          <div className="traffic-overview">

            <div className="traffic-score">

              <div className="traffic-score-ring">

                <strong>
                  {Math.round(
                    movementRate *
                      0.94,
                  )}
                </strong>

                <span>
                  / 100
                </span>

              </div>

              <div>

                <strong>
                  Network Flow
                </strong>

                <span>
                  Optimal mobility
                </span>

              </div>

            </div>

            <span className="traffic-status">
              STABLE
            </span>

          </div>

          <div className="zone-list">

            {zoneTraffic.map(
              (zone) => (
                <div
                  className="zone-row"
                  key={zone.zone}
                >

                  <div className="zone-row-top">

                    <div className="zone-name">

                      <span className="zone-dot" />

                      <strong>
                        {zone.zone}
                      </strong>

                    </div>

                    <span>
                      {zone.vehicles}
                      {" "}
                      vehicles
                    </span>

                  </div>

                  <div className="zone-progress">

                    <div
                      className={
                        zone.congestion >=
                        70
                          ? "danger"
                          : zone.congestion >=
                              40
                            ? "warning"
                            : ""
                      }
                      style={{
                        width: `${Math.max(
                          8,
                          zone.congestion,
                        )}%`,
                      }}
                    />

                  </div>

                </div>
              ),
            )}

          </div>

        </div>

      </section>

      {/* ===================================================
          LIVE FLEET
      =================================================== */}

      <section className="dashboard-panel fleet-panel">

        <PanelHeading
          eyebrow="LIVE DATA STREAM"
          title="Vehicle Command Feed"
          description="Latest simulated fleet telemetry"
          icon={
            <Zap size={18} />
          }
          action={
            <span className="panel-live-badge">

              <span />

              LIVE

            </span>
          }
        />

        <div className="fleet-table-wrapper">

          <table className="fleet-table">

            <thead>

              <tr>
                <th>VEHICLE</th>
                <th>TYPE</th>
                <th>LOCATION</th>
                <th>ZONE</th>
                <th>SPEED</th>
                <th>DIRECTION</th>
                <th>STATE</th>
              </tr>

            </thead>

            <tbody>

              {vehicles
                .slice(0, 7)
                .map(
                  (vehicle) => (
                    <tr
                      key={
                        vehicle.id
                      }
                    >

                      <td>

                        <div className="vehicle-cell">

                          <div className="vehicle-icon">
                            <CarFront
                              size={14}
                            />
                          </div>

                          <div>

                            <strong>
                              {
                                vehicle.id
                              }
                            </strong>

                            <span>
                              {
                                vehicle.plate
                              }
                            </span>

                          </div>

                        </div>

                      </td>

                      <td>
                        <span className="type-badge">
                          {
                            vehicle.type
                          }
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      <td>

                        <div className="location-cell">

                          <MapPin
                            size={13}
                          />

                          <span>
                            {
                              vehicle.road
                            }
                          </span>

                        </div>

                      </td>

                      <td>
                        {
                          vehicle.zone
                        }
                      </td>

                      <td>

                        <strong className="speed-value">
                          {
                            vehicle.speed
                          }

                          <small>
                            km/h
                          </small>
                        </strong>

                      </td>

                      <td>
                        <span className="direction-value">
                          {vehicle.direction ||
                            "N"}
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      <td>

                        <span
                          className={
                            vehicle.status ===
                            "Moving"
                              ? "fleet-status moving"
                              : "fleet-status stopped"
                          }
                        >

                          <span />

                          {
                            vehicle.status
                          }

                        </span>

                      </td>

                    </tr>
                  ),
                )}

            </tbody>

          </table>

          {!vehicles.length && (
            <div className="fleet-empty">
              No live telemetry available.
            </div>
          )}

        </div>

      </section>

      {/* ===================================================
          BOTTOM INSIGHTS
      =================================================== */}

      <section className="dashboard-bottom-grid">

        <InsightCard
          icon={
            <Route size={18} />
          }
          title="Network Coverage"
          value="87%"
          description="Road network currently represented in the digital twin."
        />

        <InsightCard
          icon={
            <CircleGauge
              size={18}
            />
          }
          title="Fleet Efficiency"
          value="92%"
          description="Simulated mobility efficiency based on active vehicles."
        />

        <InsightCard
          icon={
            <ArrowUpRight
              size={18}
            />
          }
          title="Prediction Signal"
          value="HIGH"
          description="Traffic intelligence layer is ready for scenario analysis."
        />

      </section>

      {/* ===================================================
          FOOTER
      =================================================== */}

      <footer className="dashboard-footer">

        <div>

          <Activity size={13} />

          <span>
            METROPOLIS DIGITAL TWIN
            · REAL-TIME SIMULATION
          </span>

        </div>

        <span>
          Simulation cycle:
          {" "}
          2.4 sec
        </span>

      </footer>

    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   HERO METRIC
========================================================= */

function HeroMetric({
  label,
  value,
  trend,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  trend: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="hero-metric">

      <span>
        {label}
      </span>

      <strong>
        {value}
      </strong>

      <small>
        {trend}
      </small>

    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   METRIC CARD
========================================================= */

function MetricCard({
  icon,
  label,
  value,
  suffix,
  detail,
  trend,
  accent,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  label: string;
  value: string;
  suffix?: string;
  detail: string;
  trend: string;
  accent:
    | "cyan"
    | "green"
    | "blue"
    | "amber";
}) {
  return (
    <article
      className={`metric-card accent-${accent}`}
    >

      <div className="metric-card-top">

        <div className="metric-icon">
          {icon}
        </div>

        <span className="metric-trend">
          {trend}
        </span>

      </div>

      <div className="metric-label">
        {label}
      </div>

      <div className="metric-number">

        {value}

        {suffix && (
          <small>
            {suffix}
          </small>
        )}

      </div>

      <div className="metric-detail">
        {detail}
      </div>

      <div className="metric-sparkline">

        <span />
        <span />
        <span />
        <span />
        <span />
        <span />
        <span />
        <span />

      </div>

    </article>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   PANEL HEADING
========================================================= */

function PanelHeading({
  eyebrow,
  title,
  description,
  icon,
  action,
}: {
  eyebrow: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  action?: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="advanced-panel-header">

      <div className="advanced-panel-title">

        <div className="panel-icon">
          {icon}
        </div>

        <div>

          <span>
            {eyebrow}
          </span>

          <h2>
            {title}
          </h2>

          <p>
            {description}
          </p>

        </div>

      </div>

      {action}

    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   OPERATION
========================================================= */

function Operation({
  icon,
  title,
  detail,
  value,
  good,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  title: string;
  detail: string;
  value: string;
  good: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="advanced-operation">

      <div className="advanced-operation-icon">
        {icon}
      </div>

      <div className="advanced-operation-copy">

        <strong>
          {title}
        </strong>

        <span>
          {detail}
        </span>

      </div>

      <div
        className={
          good
            ? "operation-state good"
            : "operation-state warning"
        }
      >

        <span />

        {value}

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   INSIGHT CARD
========================================================= */

function InsightCard({
  icon,
  title,
  value,
  description,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  title: string;
  value: string;
  description: string;
}) {
  return (
    <article className="insight-card">

      <div className="insight-icon">
        {icon}
      </div>

      <div className="insight-copy">

        <span>
          {title}
        </span>

        <strong>
          {value}
        </strong>

        <p>
          {description}
        </p>

      </div>

    </article>
  );
}


========== FILE: src\pages\Incidents.tsx ==========
import {
  Activity,
  AlertTriangle,
  CarFront,
  Clock,
  MapPin,
  Radio,
  ShieldAlert,
  Siren,
  TriangleAlert,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";

import { useVehicleSimulation } from "../hooks/useVehicleSimulation";

type IncidentStatus = "Active" | "Resolved";
type IncidentSeverity = "High" | "Medium" | "Low";

type Incident = {
  id: string;
  type: string;
  location: string;
  zone: string;
  severity: IncidentSeverity;
  status: IncidentStatus;
  vehicles: number;
  time: string;
  description: string;
};

export default function Incidents() {
  const { vehicles } = useVehicleSimulation();

  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<
    "All" | IncidentStatus
  >("All");

  const [severityFilter, setSeverityFilter] = useState<
    "All" | IncidentSeverity
  >("All");

  const incidents = useMemo<Incident[]>(() => {
    const stoppedVehicles = vehicles.filter(
      (vehicle) => vehicle.status === "Stopped",
    );

    const generated: Incident[] = [];

    if (stoppedVehicles.length > 0) {
      const first = stoppedVehicles[0];

      generated.push({
        id: "INC-001",
        type: "Vehicle Congestion",
        location: first.road,
        zone: first.zone,
        severity:
          stoppedVehicles.length >= 5
            ? "High"
            : stoppedVehicles.length >= 2
              ? "Medium"
              : "Low",
        status: "Active",
        vehicles: stoppedVehicles.length,
        time: "Just now",
        description:
          "Multiple stopped vehicles detected in the simulated traffic network.",
      });
    }

    if (vehicles.length > 0) {
      const slowVehicles = vehicles.filter(
        (vehicle) => vehicle.speed < 20,
      );

      if (slowVehicles.length > 0) {
        const first = slowVehicles[0];

        generated.push({
          id: "INC-002",
          type: "Slow Traffic",
          location: first.road,
          zone: first.zone,
          severity:
            slowVehicles.length >= 6
              ? "High"
              : slowVehicles.length >= 3
                ? "Medium"
                : "Low",
          status: "Active",
          vehicles: slowVehicles.length,
          time: "2 min ago",
          description:
            "Low-speed vehicle activity detected in the monitored zone.",
        });
      }
    }

    if (vehicles.length >= 5) {
      const first = vehicles[0];

      generated.push({
        id: "INC-003",
        type: "Traffic Monitoring Alert",
        location: first.road,
        zone: first.zone,
        severity: "Low",
        status: "Resolved",
        vehicles: vehicles.length,
        time: "8 min ago",
        description:
          "Traffic conditions are being monitored by the digital twin engine.",
      });
    }

    return generated;
  }, [vehicles]);

  const filteredIncidents = incidents.filter((incident) => {
    const matchesStatus =
      statusFilter === "All" ||
      incident.status === statusFilter;

    const matchesSeverity =
      severityFilter === "All" ||
      incident.severity === severityFilter;

    return matchesStatus && matchesSeverity;
  });

  const activeCount = incidents.filter(
    (incident) => incident.status === "Active",
  ).length;

  const highCount = incidents.filter(
    (incident) => incident.severity === "High",
  ).length;

  const resolvedCount = incidents.filter(
    (incident) => incident.status === "Resolved",
  ).length;

  const getSeverityClass = (
    severity: IncidentSeverity,
  ) => {
    if (severity === "High") {
      return "status status-high";
    }

    if (severity === "Medium") {
      return "status status-medium";
    }

    return "status status-low";
  };

  const getStatusClass = (
    status: IncidentStatus,
  ) => {
    return status === "Active"
      ? "status status-medium"
      : "status status-low";
  };

  return (
    <div className="metropolis-page">
      {/* HEADER */}

      <div className="page-header">
        <div>
          <h1 className="page-title">
            Incident Intelligence
          </h1>

          <p className="page-subtitle">
            Real-time simulated incident detection,
            monitoring and response management
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="live-pill">
          <span className="live-dot" />
          INCIDENT MONITORING
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* KPI CARDS */}

      <div className="grid-4">
        <IncidentCard
          icon={<TriangleAlert size={21} />}
          label="Total Incidents"
          value={incidents.length.toString()}
          description="Detected by simulation"
        />

        <IncidentCard
          icon={<Siren size={21} />}
          label="Active"
          value={activeCount.toString()}
          description="Currently requiring attention"
        />

        <IncidentCard
          icon={<ShieldAlert size={21} />}
          label="High Severity"
          value={highCount.toString()}
          description="Priority incidents"
        />

        <IncidentCard
          icon={<Activity size={21} />}
          label="Resolved"
          value={resolvedCount.toString()}
          description="Previously monitored"
        />
      </div>

      {/* OVERVIEW */}

      <div
        className="grid-2"
        style={{
          marginTop: 18,
          alignItems: "start",
        }}
      >
        {/* INCIDENT ENGINE */}

        <section className="panel">
          <div className="panel-header">
            <div>
              <h3 className="panel-title">
                Incident Detection Engine
              </h3>

              <p
                className="muted"
                style={{
                  marginTop: 5,
                  fontSize: 12,
                }}
              >
                Current digital twin monitoring status
              </p>
            </div>

            <Radio size={18} />
          </div>

          <div style={{ padding: 18 }}>
            <IncidentStatusRow
              icon={<Radio size={16} />}
              title="Detection Engine"
              value="Active"
              good
            />

            <IncidentStatusRow
              icon={<Activity size={16} />}
              title="Vehicle Stream"
              value="Connected"
              good
            />

            <IncidentStatusRow
              icon={<TriangleAlert size={16} />}
              title="Incident Monitoring"
              value="Running"
              good
            />

            <IncidentStatusRow
              icon={<CarFront size={16} />}
              title="Vehicles Monitored"
              value={vehicles.length.toString()}
              good
            />
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* SEVERITY SUMMARY */}

        <section className="panel">
          <div className="panel-header">
            <div>
              <h3 className="panel-title">
                Severity Summary
              </h3>

              <p
                className="muted"
                style={{
                  marginTop: 5,
                  fontSize: 12,
                }}
              >
                Current incident priority distribution
              </p>
            </div>

            <ShieldAlert size={18} />
          </div>

          <div style={{ padding: 18 }}>
            <SeverityRow
              label="High"
              value={
                incidents.filter(
                  (incident) =>
                    incident.severity === "High",
                ).length
              }
              total={incidents.length}
            />

            <SeverityRow
              label="Medium"
              value={
                incidents.filter(
                  (incident) =>
                    incident.severity === "Medium",
                ).length
              }
              total={incidents.length}
            />

            <SeverityRow
              label="Low"
              value={
                incidents.filter(
                  (incident) =>
                    incident.severity === "Low",
                ).length
              }
              total={incidents.length}
            />
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>

      {/* FILTERS */}

      <section
        className="panel"
        style={{
          marginTop: 18,
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            padding: 16,
            display: "flex",
            alignItems: "center",
            gap: 8,
            flexWrap: "wrap",
          }}
        >
          <span
            className="muted"
            style={{
              fontSize: 10,
              marginRight: 4,
            }}
          >
            STATUS
          </span>

          <FilterButton
            active={statusFilter === "All"}
            onClick={() => setStatusFilter("All")}
          >
            All
          </FilterButton>

          <FilterButton
            active={statusFilter === "Active"}
            onClick={() =>
              setStatusFilter("Active")
            }
          >
            Active
          </FilterButton>

          <FilterButton
            active={statusFilter === "Resolved"}
            onClick={() =>
              setStatusFilter("Resolved")
            }
          >
            Resolved
          </FilterButton>

          <div
            style={{
              width: 1,
              height: 24,
              background:
                "rgba(100,160,190,.12)",
              margin: "0 6px",
            }}
          />

          <span
            className="muted"
            style={{
              fontSize: 10,
              marginRight: 4,
            }}
          >
            SEVERITY
          </span>

          <FilterButton
            active={severityFilter === "All"}
            onClick={() =>
              setSeverityFilter("All")
            }
          >
            All
          </FilterButton>

          <FilterButton
            active={severityFilter === "High"}
            onClick={() =>
              setSeverityFilter("High")
            }
          >
            High
          </FilterButton>

          <FilterButton
            active={severityFilter === "Medium"}
            onClick={() =>
              setSeverityFilter("Medium")
            }
          >
            Medium
          </FilterButton>

          <FilterButton
            active={severityFilter === "Low"}
            onClick={() =>
              setSeverityFilter("Low")
            }
          >
            Low
          </FilterButton>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* INCIDENT REGISTRY */}

      <section
        className="panel"
        style={{
          marginTop: 18,
        }}
      >
        <div className="panel-header">
          <div>
            <h3 className="panel-title">
              Incident Registry
            </h3>

            <p
              className="muted"
              style={{
                marginTop: 5,
                fontSize: 12,
              }}
            >
              {filteredIncidents.length} incidents
              matching the selected filters
            </p>
          </div>

          <div
            style={{
              display: "flex",
              alignItems: "center",
              gap: 7,
            }}
          >
            <AlertTriangle size={15} />

            <span className="status status-low">
              MONITORING
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            overflowX: "auto",
          }}
        >
          <table
            style={{
              width: "100%",
              minWidth: 950,
              borderCollapse: "collapse",
            }}
          >
            <thead>
              <tr
                style={{
                  color: "#688493",
                  fontSize: 10,
                  textTransform: "uppercase",
                  letterSpacing: ".06em",
                }}
              >
                <th style={tableHeader}>
                  Incident
                </th>

                <th style={tableHeader}>
                  Type
                </th>

                <th style={tableHeader}>
                  Location
                </th>

                <th style={tableHeader}>
                  Zone
                </th>

                <th style={tableHeader}>
                  Vehicles
                </th>

                <th style={tableHeader}>
                  Severity
                </th>

                <th style={tableHeader}>
                  Status
                </th>

                <th style={tableHeader}>
                  Time
                </th>
              </tr>
            </thead>

            <tbody>
              {filteredIncidents.length > 0 ? (
                filteredIncidents.map(
                  (incident) => (
                    <tr key={incident.id}>
                      <td style={tableCell}>
                        <div
                          style={{
                            display: "flex",
