                              background:
                                "rgba(239,106,120,.07)",
                            }}
                          >
                            <TriangleAlert
                              size={16}
                            />
                          </div>

                          <div>
                            <strong
                              style={{
                                fontSize: 11,
                              }}
                            >
                              {incident.id}
                            </strong>

                            <div
                              className="muted"
                              style={{
                                fontSize: 9,
                                marginTop: 3,
                              }}
                            >
                              Incident ID
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </td>

                      <td style={tableCell}>
                        <strong
                          style={{
                            fontSize: 11,
                          }}
                        >
                          {incident.type}
                        </strong>

                        <div
                          className="muted"
                          style={{
                            fontSize: 9,
                            marginTop: 3,
                            maxWidth: 230,
                          }}
                        >
                          {incident.description}
                        </div>
                      </td>

                      <td style={tableCell}>
                        <div
                          style={{
                            display: "flex",
                            alignItems: "center",
                            gap: 5,
                          }}
                        >
                          <MapPin size={13} />
                          {incident.location}
                        </div>
                      </td>

                      <td style={tableCell}>
                        <span className="status status-medium">
                          {incident.zone}
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      <td style={tableCell}>
                        <div
                          style={{
                            display: "flex",
                            alignItems: "center",
                            gap: 5,
                          }}
                        >
                          <CarFront size={13} />
                          {incident.vehicles}
                        </div>
                      </td>

                      <td style={tableCell}>
                        <span
                          className={getSeverityClass(
                            incident.severity,
                          )}
                        >
                          {incident.severity}
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      <td style={tableCell}>
                        <span
                          className={getStatusClass(
                            incident.status,
                          )}
                        >
                          {incident.status}
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      <td style={tableCell}>
                        <div
                          style={{
                            display: "flex",
                            alignItems: "center",
                            gap: 5,
                          }}
                        >
                          <Clock size={13} />
                          {incident.time}
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ),
                )
              ) : (
                <tr>
                  <td
                    colSpan={8}
                    style={{
                      padding: "45px 20px",
                      textAlign: "center",
                      color: "#64808d",
                      fontSize: 12,
                    }}
                  >
                    No incidents found
                    matching the selected
                    filters.
                  </td>
                </tr>
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* RESPONSE STATUS */}

      <div
        className="grid-2"
        style={{
          marginTop: 18,
          alignItems: "start",
        }}
      >
        <section className="panel">
          <div className="panel-header">
            <div>
              <h3 className="panel-title">
                Response Status
              </h3>

              <p
                className="muted"
                style={{
                  marginTop: 5,
                  fontSize: 12,
                }}
              >
                Automated incident response readiness
              </p>
            </div>

            <Siren size={18} />
          </div>

          <div style={{ padding: 18 }}>
            <ResponseRow
              title="Incident Detection"
              value="Online"
              good
            />

            <ResponseRow
              title="Traffic Monitoring"
              value="Active"
              good
            />

            <ResponseRow
              title="Vehicle Tracking"
              value="Connected"
              good
            />

            <ResponseRow
              title="Alert Processing"
              value="Ready"
              good
            />
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="panel">
          <div className="panel-header">
            <div>
              <h3 className="panel-title">
                Incident Summary
              </h3>

              <p
                className="muted"
                style={{
                  marginTop: 5,
                  fontSize: 12,
                }}
              >
                Current digital twin statistics
              </p>
            </div>

            <Activity size={18} />
          </div>

          <div style={{ padding: 18 }}>
            <SummaryItem
              label="Total Incidents"
              value={incidents.length}
            />

            <SummaryItem
              label="Active Incidents"
              value={activeCount}
            />

            <SummaryItem
              label="High Severity"
              value={highCount}
            />

            <SummaryItem
              label="Resolved Incidents"
              value={resolvedCount}
            />
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>

      {/* FOOTER */}

      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          gap: 8,
          marginTop: 14,
          paddingBottom: 20,
          color: "#64808d",
          fontSize: 10,
        }}
      >
        <Activity size={13} />

        METROPOLIS incident intelligence engine
        continuously monitors simulated traffic
        activity and generates alerts.
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   INCIDENT CARD
========================================================= */

function IncidentCard({
  icon,
  label,
  value,
  description,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  label: string;
  value: string;
  description: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="panel kpi-card">
      {icon}

      <div
        className="kpi-label"
        style={{
          marginTop: 14,
        }}
      >
        {label}
      </div>

      <div className="kpi-value">
        {value}
      </div>

      <div className="kpi-change">
        {description}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   FILTER BUTTON
========================================================= */

function FilterButton({
  active,
  children,
  onClick,
}: {
  active: boolean;
  children: React.ReactNode;
  onClick: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClick}
      style={{
        border: active
          ? "1px solid rgba(20,190,235,.35)"
          : "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.10)",
        background: active
          ? "rgba(20,190,235,.09)"
          : "rgba(100,160,190,.035)",
        color: active
          ? "#d8f8ff"
          : "#78929e",
        borderRadius: 7,
        padding: "8px 14px",
        fontSize: 10,
        fontWeight: 600,
        cursor: "pointer",
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   INCIDENT STATUS ROW
========================================================= */

function IncidentStatusRow({
  icon,
  title,
  value,
  good,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  title: string;
  value: string;
  good: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: "flex",
        alignItems: "center",
        gap: 12,
        padding: "12px 0",
        borderBottom:
          "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.07)",
      }}
    >
      <div
        style={{
          width: 32,
          height: 32,
          borderRadius: 7,
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          justifyContent: "center",
          background: good
            ? "rgba(49,210,154,.07)"
            : "rgba(239,106,120,.07)",
        }}
      >
        {icon}
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          flex: 1,
          fontSize: 11,
          fontWeight: 600,
        }}
      >
        {title}
      </div>

      <span
        className={
          good
            ? "status status-low"
            : "status status-medium"
        }
      >
        {value}
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   SEVERITY ROW
========================================================= */

function SeverityRow({
  label,
  value,
  total,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: number;
  total: number;
}) {
  const percentage =
    total > 0
      ? Math.round((value / total) * 100)
      : 0;

  const background =
    label === "High"
      ? "#ef6a78"
      : label === "Medium"
        ? "#e5b45b"
        : "#31d29a";

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        marginBottom: 18,
      }}
    >
      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          justifyContent: "space-between",
          marginBottom: 7,
        }}
      >
        <span
          style={{
            fontSize: 11,
            fontWeight: 600,
          }}
        >
          {label}
        </span>

        <span
          className="muted"
          style={{
            fontSize: 10,
          }}
        >
          {value} incidents · {percentage}%
        </span>
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          height: 7,
          borderRadius: 20,
          background:
            "rgba(100,160,190,.10)",
          overflow: "hidden",
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            width: `${percentage}%`,
            height: "100%",
            borderRadius: 20,
            background,
            transition: "width .3s ease",
          }}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   RESPONSE ROW
========================================================= */

function ResponseRow({
  title,
  value,
  good,
}: {
  title: string;
  value: string;
  good: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: "flex",
        justifyContent: "space-between",
        alignItems: "center",
        padding: "12px 0",
        borderBottom:
          "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.07)",
      }}
    >
      <span
        style={{
          fontSize: 11,
          fontWeight: 600,
        }}
      >
        {title}
      </span>

      <span
        className={
          good
            ? "status status-low"
            : "status status-medium"
        }
      >
        {value}
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   SUMMARY ITEM
========================================================= */

function SummaryItem({
  label,
  value,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string | number;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: "flex",
        justifyContent: "space-between",
        alignItems: "center",
        padding: "12px 0",
        borderBottom:
          "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.07)",
      }}
    >
      <span
        className="muted"
        style={{
          fontSize: 10,
        }}
      >
        {label}
      </span>

      <strong
        style={{
          fontSize: 12,
        }}
      >
        {value}
      </strong>
    </div>
  );
}

const tableHeader: React.CSSProperties = {
  textAlign: "left",
  padding: "13px 16px",

    "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.08)",
};

const tableCell: React.CSSProperties = {

    "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.06)",
  fontSize: 11,
};

import {
  Activity,
  CarFront,
  LocateFixed,
  Navigation,
  Radio,
  RefreshCw,
  Search,
  Signal,
  Zap,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";

import { useVehicleSimulation } from "../hooks/useVehicleSimulation";

export default function LiveMap() {
  const { vehicles } = useVehicleSimulation();

  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [selectedVehicleId, setSelectedVehicleId] =
    useState<string | null>(null);

  const filteredVehicles = useMemo(() => {
    const query = search.trim().toLowerCase();

    if (!query) {
      return vehicles;
    }

    return vehicles.filter((vehicle) =>
      [
        vehicle.id,
        vehicle.plate,
        vehicle.type,
        vehicle.road,
        vehicle.zone,
        vehicle.status,
      ]
        .join(" ")
        .toLowerCase()
        .includes(query),
    );
  }, [vehicles, search]);

  const selectedVehicle = vehicles.find(
    (vehicle) => vehicle.id === selectedVehicleId,
  );

  const movingVehicles = vehicles.filter(
    (vehicle) => vehicle.status === "Moving",
  ).length;

  const stoppedVehicles = vehicles.filter(
    (vehicle) => vehicle.status === "Stopped",
  ).length;

  return (
    <div className="metropolis-page">
      {/* =================================================
          HEADER
      ================================================= */}

      <div className="page-header">
        <div>
          <h1 className="page-title">
            Live Digital Twin
          </h1>

          <p className="page-subtitle">
            Real-time simulated vehicle tracking and
            smart-city traffic visualization
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="live-pill">
          <span className="live-dot" />
          LIVE SIMULATION
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* =================================================
          TOP STATUS
      ================================================= */}

      <div className="grid-4">
        <MapCard
          icon={<CarFront size={21} />}
          label="Tracked Vehicles"
          value={vehicles.length.toString()}
          description="Active simulation objects"
        />

        <MapCard
          icon={<Navigation size={21} />}
          label="Moving"
          value={movingVehicles.toString()}
          description="Vehicles in motion"
        />

        <MapCard
          icon={<LocateFixed size={21} />}
          label="Stopped"
          value={stoppedVehicles.toString()}
          description="Stationary vehicles"
        />

        <MapCard
          icon={<Signal size={21} />}
          label="Connection"
          value="LIVE"
          description="Simulation engine connected"
        />
      </div>

      {/* =================================================
          MAIN MAP AREA
      ================================================= */}

      <section
        className="panel"
        style={{
          marginTop: 18,
          overflow: "hidden",
        }}
      >
        {/* MAP HEADER */}

        <div
          style={{
            display: "flex",
            alignItems: "center",
            justifyContent: "space-between",
            gap: 15,
            padding: "14px 18px",
            borderBottom:
              "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.08)",
            flexWrap: "wrap",
          }}
        >
          <div>
            <h3 className="panel-title">
              METROPOLIS City Map
            </h3>

            <p
              className="muted"
              style={{
                marginTop: 4,
                fontSize: 10,
              }}
            >
              Simulated real-time vehicle positions
            </p>
          </div>

          <div
            style={{
              display: "flex",
              alignItems: "center",
              gap: 8,
            }}
          >
            <div
              style={{
                display: "flex",
                alignItems: "center",
                gap: 6,
                color: "#6d8794",
                fontSize: 9,
              }}
            >
              <span
                style={{
                  width: 7,
                  height: 7,
                  borderRadius: "50%",
                  background: "#31d29a",
                }}
              />

              Moving
            </div>

            <div
              style={{
                display: "flex",
                alignItems: "center",
                gap: 6,
                color: "#6d8794",
                fontSize: 9,
              }}
            >
              <span
                style={{
                  width: 7,
                  height: 7,
                  borderRadius: "50%",
                  background: "#e5b45b",
                }}
              />

              Stopped
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* MAP */}

        <div
          style={{
            position: "relative",
            height: 540,
            overflow: "hidden",
            background:
              "radial-gradient(circle at 50% 50%, rgba(20,190,235,.08), transparent 45%), #071218",
          }}
        >
          {/* GRID */}

          <div
            style={{
              position: "absolute",
              inset: 0,
              opacity: 0.18,
              backgroundImage:
                "linear-gradient(rgba(90,170,195,.12) 1px, transparent 1px), linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(90,170,195,.12) 1px, transparent 1px)",
              backgroundSize:
                "42px 42px",
            }}
          />

          {/* ROAD NETWORK */}

          <div
            style={{
              position: "absolute",
              left: "8%",
              right: "8%",
              top: "22%",
              height: 3,
              background:
                "rgba(90,160,180,.25)",
              boxShadow:
                "0 0 14px rgba(20,190,235,.10)",
              transform:
                "rotate(-7deg)",
            }}
          />

          <div
            style={{
              position: "absolute",
              left: "10%",
              right: "10%",
              top: "50%",
              height: 3,
              background:
                "rgba(90,160,180,.25)",
              boxShadow:
                "0 0 14px rgba(20,190,235,.10)",
            }}
          />

          <div
            style={{
              position: "absolute",
              left: "12%",
              right: "12%",
              top: "76%",
              height: 3,
              background:
                "rgba(90,160,180,.25)",
              transform:
                "rotate(6deg)",
            }}
          />

          <div
            style={{
              position: "absolute",
              top: "8%",
              bottom: "8%",
              left: "30%",
              width: 3,
              background:
                "rgba(90,160,180,.20)",
              transform:
                "rotate(4deg)",
            }}
          />

          <div
            style={{
              position: "absolute",
              top: "8%",
              bottom: "8%",
              left: "62%",
              width: 3,
              background:
                "rgba(90,160,180,.20)",
              transform:
                "rotate(-5deg)",
            }}
          />

          <div
            style={{
              position: "absolute",
              top: "12%",
              bottom: "12%",
              left: "82%",
              width: 3,
              background:
                "rgba(90,160,180,.16)",
            }}
          />

          {/* ZONES */}

          <ZoneLabel
            name="ZONE A"
            top="15%"
            left="14%"
          />

          <ZoneLabel
            name="ZONE B"
            top="43%"
            left="42%"
          />

          <ZoneLabel
            name="ZONE C"
            top="68%"
            left="70%"
          />

          {/* VEHICLE MARKERS */}

          {filteredVehicles.map(
            (vehicle, index) => {
              const left =
                14 +
                ((index * 17) % 72);

              const top =
                15 +
                ((index * 23) % 68);

              const isSelected =
                selectedVehicleId ===
                vehicle.id;

              return (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  key={vehicle.id}
                  onClick={() =>
                    setSelectedVehicleId(
                      vehicle.id,
                    )
                  }
                  title={`${vehicle.id} • ${vehicle.speed} km/h`}
                  style={{
                    position:
                      "absolute",
                    left: `${left}%`,
                    top: `${top}%`,
                    transform:
                      "translate(-50%, -50%)",
                    width: isSelected
                      ? 34
                      : 27,
                    height: isSelected
                      ? 34
                      : 27,
                    borderRadius: "50%",
                    border: isSelected
                      ? "2px solid rgba(255,255,255,.85)"
                      : "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.22)",
                    background:
                      vehicle.status ===
                      "Moving"
                        ? "rgba(49,210,154,.88)"
                        : "rgba(229,180,91,.90)",
                    color: "#061015",
                    display: "flex",
                    alignItems:
                      "center",
                    justifyContent:
                      "center",
                    cursor: "pointer",
                    boxShadow:
                      vehicle.status ===
                      "Moving"
                        ? "0 0 18px rgba(49,210,154,.42)"
                        : "0 0 18px rgba(229,180,91,.35)",
                    zIndex:
                      isSelected ? 10 : 3,
                  }}
                >
                  <CarFront
                    size={
                      isSelected
                        ? 16
                        : 13
                    }
                  />
                </button>
              );
            },
          )}

          {/* SEARCH */}

          <div
            style={{
              position:
                "absolute",
              top: 16,
              left: 16,
              width: 270,
              zIndex: 20,
            }}
          >
            <div
              style={{
                display:
                  "flex",
                alignItems:
                  "center",
                gap: 8,
                padding:
                  "10px 12px",
                border:
                  "1px solid rgba(100,180,200,.15)",
                borderRadius: 8,
                background:
                  "rgba(5,15,20,.88)",
                backdropFilter:
                  "blur(10px)",
              }}
            >
              <Search
                size={14}
                color="#6f8d99"
              />

              <input
                value={search}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setSearch(
                    event.target
                      .value,
                  )
                }
                placeholder="Search vehicle, plate, road..."
                style={{
                  width: "100%",
                  border: "none",
                  outline: "none",
                  background:
                    "transparent",

                    "#d9edf2",
                  fontSize: 10,
                }}
              />

            {search &&

                0 && (

                            setSelectedVehicleId(
                              vehicle.id,
                            );
                            setSearch(
                              vehicle.id,
                            );
                          }}
                          style={{
                            width: "100%",
                            border:
                              "none",
                            background:
                              "transparent",
                            color:
                              "#c8e0e7",
                            padding:
                              "9px 11px",

                        >

                              marginLeft: 7,
                            }}

                            {
                              vehicle.road
                            }
                          </span>
                        </button>

                    )}

              )}
          </div>

          {/* MAP CONTROLS */}

                  "blur(12px)",

                    }

                      marginTop: 3,

                    }}

                    }

            </h3>

              className="muted"

                marginTop: 5,
                fontSize: 12,
              }}

              Select a vehicle to inspect its
              current simulation state
            </p>
          </div>

          <span className="status status-low">
            {filteredVehicles.length} TRACKED
          </span>
        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            overflowX:
              "auto",
          }}
        >
          <table
            style={{
              width: "100%",
              minWidth: 760,
              borderCollapse:
                "collapse",
            }}
          >
            <thead>
              <tr
                style={{
                  color:
                    "#688493",
                  fontSize: 10,
                  textTransform:
                    "uppercase",
                  letterSpacing:
                    ".06em",
                }}
              >
                <th
                  style={
                    tableHeader
                  }
                >
                  Vehicle
                </th>

                <th
                  style={
                    tableHeader
                  }
                >
                  Plate
                </th>

                <th
                  style={
                    tableHeader
                  }
                >
                  Type
                </th>

                <th
                  style={
                    tableHeader
                  }
                >
                  Road
                </th>

                <th
                  style={
                    tableHeader
                  }
                >
                  Zone
                </th>

                <th
                  style={
                    tableHeader
                  }
                >
                  Speed
                </th>

                <th
                  style={
                    tableHeader
                  }
                >
                  Status
                </th>
              </tr>
            </thead>

            <tbody>
              {filteredVehicles
                .slice(0, 10)
                .map(
                  (vehicle) => (
                    <tr
                      key={
                        vehicle.id
                      }
                      onClick={() =>
                        setSelectedVehicleId(
                          vehicle.id,
                        )
                      }
                      style={{
                        cursor:
                          "pointer",
                      }}
                    >
                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        <strong>
                          {
                            vehicle.id
                          }
                        </strong>
                      </td>

                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        {
                          vehicle.plate
                        }
                      </td>

                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        {
                          vehicle.type
                        }
                      </td>

                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        {
                          vehicle.road
                        }
                      </td>

                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        {
                          vehicle.zone
                        }
                      </td>

                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        {
                          vehicle.speed
                        }{" "}
                        km/h
                      </td>

                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        <span
                          className={
                            vehicle.status ===
                            "Moving"
                              ? "status status-low"
                              : "status status-medium"
                          }
                        >
                          {
                            vehicle.status
                          }
                        </span>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ),
                )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* =================================================
          FOOTER
      ================================================= */}

      <div
        style={{
          display:
            "flex",
          alignItems:
            "center",
          gap: 8,
          marginTop: 14,
          paddingBottom: 20,
          color:
            "#64808d",
          fontSize: 10,
        }}
      >
        <Activity
          size={13}
        />

        METROPOLIS digital twin continuously
        visualizes simulated vehicle movement,
        locations and traffic conditions.
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

          fontSize: 10,
          fontWeight: 600,
          whiteSpace:
            "nowrap",
          overflow:
            "hidden",
          textOverflow:
            "ellipsis",
        }}
      >
        {value}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

========== FILE: src\pages\Reports.tsx ==========
import {
  Activity,
  BarChart3,
  CarFront,
  Download,
  FileText,
  Gauge,
  TrendingUp,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useMemo } from "react";

import { useVehicleSimulation } from "../hooks/useVehicleSimulation";

export default function Reports() {
  const { vehicles } = useVehicleSimulation();

  const totalVehicles = vehicles.length;

  const movingVehicles = vehicles.filter(
    (vehicle) => vehicle.status === "Moving",
  ).length;

  const stoppedVehicles = vehicles.filter(
    (vehicle) => vehicle.status === "Stopped",
  ).length;

  const averageSpeed = useMemo(() => {
    if (vehicles.length === 0) {
      return 0;
    }

    return Math.round(
      vehicles.reduce(
        (total, vehicle) => total + vehicle.speed,
        0,
      ) / vehicles.length,
    );
  }, [vehicles]);

  const fastestVehicle = useMemo(() => {
    if (vehicles.length === 0) {
      return null;
    }

    return vehicles.reduce((fastest, vehicle) =>
      vehicle.speed > fastest.speed
        ? vehicle
        : fastest,
    );
  }, [vehicles]);

  const zoneCounts = useMemo(() => {
    const counts: Record<string, number> = {};

    vehicles.forEach((vehicle) => {
      counts[vehicle.zone] =
        (counts[vehicle.zone] || 0) + 1;
    });

    return counts;
  }, [vehicles]);

  const movingPercentage =
    totalVehicles > 0
      ? Math.round(
          (movingVehicles / totalVehicles) * 100,
        )
      : 0;

  const stoppedPercentage =
    totalVehicles > 0
      ? Math.round(
          (stoppedVehicles / totalVehicles) * 100,
        )
      : 0;

  return (
    <div className="metropolis-page">
      {/* =================================================
          HEADER
      ================================================= */}

      <div className="page-header">
        <div>
          <h1 className="page-title">
            Operations Reports
          </h1>

          <p className="page-subtitle">
            Smart-city simulation performance and
            operational intelligence reports
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          className="report-button"
          onClick={() => {
            window.print();
          }}
        >
          <Download size={15} />
          Export Report
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* =================================================
          SUMMARY CARDS
      ================================================= */}

      <div className="grid-4">
        <ReportCard
          icon={<CarFront size={21} />}
          label="Total Vehicles"
          value={totalVehicles.toString()}
          description="Currently simulated"
        />

        <ReportCard
          icon={<Activity size={21} />}
          label="Moving Rate"
          value={`${movingPercentage}%`}
          description={`${movingVehicles} vehicles moving`}
        />

        <ReportCard
          icon={<Gauge size={21} />}
          label="Average Speed"
          value={`${averageSpeed} km/h`}
          description="Across all vehicles"
        />

        <ReportCard
          icon={<TrendingUp size={21} />}
          label="Fastest Vehicle"
          value={
            fastestVehicle
              ? `${fastestVehicle.speed} km/h`
              : "0 km/h"
          }
          description={
            fastestVehicle
              ? fastestVehicle.id
              : "No vehicle data"
          }
        />
      </div>

      {/* =================================================
          PERFORMANCE OVERVIEW
      ================================================= */}

      <div
        className="grid-2"
        style={{
          marginTop: 18,
          alignItems: "start",
        }}
      >
        {/* TRAFFIC PERFORMANCE */}

        <section className="panel">
          <div className="panel-header">
            <div>
              <h3 className="panel-title">
                Traffic Performance
              </h3>

              <p
                className="muted"
                style={{
                  marginTop: 5,
                  fontSize: 12,
                }}
              >
                Current vehicle movement statistics
              </p>
            </div>

            <BarChart3 size={18} />
          </div>

          <div style={{ padding: 18 }}>
            <PerformanceRow
              label="Moving Vehicles"
              value={movingVehicles}
              total={totalVehicles}
              percentage={movingPercentage}
              type="good"
            />

            <PerformanceRow
              label="Stopped Vehicles"
              value={stoppedVehicles}
              total={totalVehicles}
              percentage={stoppedPercentage}
              type="warning"
            />

            <PerformanceRow
              label="Average Speed"
              value={averageSpeed}
              total={60}
              percentage={Math.min(
                Math.round(
                  (averageSpeed / 60) * 100,
                ),
                100,
              )}
              type="good"
              suffix=" km/h"
            />
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* SYSTEM SUMMARY */}

        <section className="panel">
          <div className="panel-header">
            <div>
              <h3 className="panel-title">
                System Summary
              </h3>

              <p
                className="muted"
                style={{
                  marginTop: 5,
                  fontSize: 12,
                }}
              >
                Current digital twin status
              </p>
            </div>

            <FileText size={18} />
          </div>

          <div style={{ padding: 18 }}>
            <SummaryRow
              label="Simulation Status"
              value="Running"
              status="good"
            />

            <SummaryRow
              label="Vehicle Engine"
              value="Connected"
              status="good"
            />

            <SummaryRow
              label="Traffic Engine"
              value="Active"
              status="good"
            />

            <SummaryRow
              label="Tracked Zones"
              value="3"
              status="good"
            />

            <SummaryRow
              label="System Mode"
              value="Digital Twin"
              status="good"
            />
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>

      {/* =================================================
          ZONE ANALYSIS
      ================================================= */}

      <section
        className="panel"
        style={{
          marginTop: 18,
        }}
      >
        <div className="panel-header">
          <div>
            <h3 className="panel-title">
              Zone Performance Report
            </h3>

            <p
              className="muted"
              style={{
                marginTop: 5,
                fontSize: 12,
              }}
            >
              Simulated vehicle distribution across
              operational zones
            </p>
          </div>

          <Activity size={18} />
        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            padding: 18,
            display: "grid",
            gridTemplateColumns:
              "repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr))",
            gap: 14,
          }}
        >
          <ZoneReport
            name="Zone A"
            vehicles={
              zoneCounts["Zone A"] || 0
            }
            percentage={65}
            description="Normal traffic"
          />

          <ZoneReport
            name="Zone B"
            vehicles={
              zoneCounts["Zone B"] || 0
            }
            percentage={82}
            description="High traffic"
          />

          <ZoneReport
            name="Zone C"
            vehicles={
              zoneCounts["Zone C"] || 0
            }
            percentage={32}
            description="Low traffic"
          />
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* =================================================
          VEHICLE REPORT
      ================================================= */}

      <section
        className="panel"
        style={{
          marginTop: 18,
        }}
      >
        <div className="panel-header">
          <div>
            <h3 className="panel-title">
              Vehicle Performance Report
            </h3>

            <p
              className="muted"
              style={{
                marginTop: 5,
                fontSize: 12,
              }}
            >
              Detailed operational status of simulated
              vehicles
            </p>
          </div>

          <span className="status status-low">
            {vehicles.length} VEHICLES
          </span>
        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            overflowX: "auto",
          }}
        >
          <table
            style={{
              width: "100%",
              minWidth: 780,
              borderCollapse: "collapse",
            }}
          >
            <thead>
              <tr
                style={{
                  color: "#688493",
                  fontSize: 10,
                  textTransform: "uppercase",
                  letterSpacing: ".06em",
                }}
              >
                <th style={tableHeader}>
                  Vehicle
                </th>

                <th style={tableHeader}>
                  Type
                </th>

                <th style={tableHeader}>
                  Road
                </th>

                <th style={tableHeader}>
                  Zone
                </th>

                <th style={tableHeader}>
                  Speed
                </th>

                <th style={tableHeader}>
                  Status
                </th>

                <th style={tableHeader}>
                  Performance
                </th>
              </tr>
            </thead>

            <tbody>
              {vehicles.slice(0, 10).map(
                (vehicle) => {
                  const performance =
                    vehicle.status ===
                    "Moving"
                      ? vehicle.speed >= 40
                        ? "High"
                        : "Normal"
                      : "Low";

                  return (
                    <tr key={vehicle.id}>
                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        <strong>
                          {vehicle.id}
                        </strong>
                      </td>

                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        {vehicle.type}
                      </td>

                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        {vehicle.road}
                      </td>

                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        {vehicle.zone}
                      </td>

                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        {vehicle.speed}{" "}
                        km/h
                      </td>

                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        <span
                          className={
                            vehicle.status ===
                            "Moving"
                              ? "status status-low"
                              : "status status-medium"
                          }
                        >
                          {
                            vehicle.status
                          }
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        <span
                          className={
                            performance ===
                            "High"
                              ? "status status-low"
                              : performance ===
                                  "Normal"
                                ? "status status-medium"
                                : "status status-high"
                          }
                        >
                          {performance}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                },
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* =================================================
          REPORT FOOTER
      ================================================= */}

      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          gap: 8,
          marginTop: 14,
          paddingBottom: 20,
          color: "#64808d",
          fontSize: 10,
        }}
      >
        <Activity size={13} />

        METROPOLIS reporting engine is using the
        continuously simulated vehicle dataset.
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function ReportCard({
  icon,
  label,

}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  label: string;
  value: string;

        style={{
          marginTop: 14,
        }}

      </div>

/* =========================================================
   PERFORMANCE ROW
========================================================= */

function PerformanceRow({
  label,
  value,
  total,
  percentage,
  type,
  suffix = "",
}: {
  label: string;
  value: number;
  total: number;
  percentage: number;
  type: "good" | "warning";
  suffix?: string;
}) {
  const safePercentage = Math.max(
    0,
    Math.min(percentage, 100),
  );

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        marginBottom: 20,
      }}
    >
      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          justifyContent: "space-between",
          marginBottom: 7,
        }}
      >
        <span
          style={{
            fontSize: 11,
            fontWeight: 600,
          }}
        >
          {label}
        </span>

        <span
          className="muted"
          style={{
            fontSize: 10,
          }}
        >
          {value}
          {suffix}{" "}
          {suffix === ""
            ? `/ ${total}`
            : ""}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          height: 7,
          borderRadius: 20,
          background:
            "rgba(100,160,190,.10)",
          overflow: "hidden",
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            width: `${safePercentage}%`,
            height: "100%",
            borderRadius: 20,
            background:
              type === "warning"
                ? "#e5b45b"
                : "#31d29a",
            transition:
              "width .3s ease",
          }}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   SUMMARY ROW
========================================================= */

function SummaryRow({
  label,
  value,
  status,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  status: "good" | "warning";
}) {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: "flex",
        alignItems: "center",
        gap: 12,
        padding: "12px 0",
        borderBottom:
          "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.07)",
      }}
    >
      <div
        style={{
          width: 8,
          height: 8,
          borderRadius: "50%",
          background:
            status === "good"
              ? "#31d29a"
              : "#e5b45b",
          boxShadow:
            status === "good"
              ? "0 0 10px rgba(49,210,154,.45)"
              : "0 0 10px rgba(229,180,91,.35)",
        }}
      />

      <div
        style={{
          flex: 1,
          fontSize: 11,
          fontWeight: 600,
        }}
      >
        {label}
      </div>

      <span
        className={
          status === "good"
            ? "status status-low"
            : "status status-medium"
        }
      >
        {value}
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   ZONE REPORT
========================================================= */

function ZoneReport({
  name,
  vehicles,
  percentage,
  description,
}: {
  name: string;
  vehicles: number;
  percentage: number;
  description: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        padding: 16,
        border:
          "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.09)",
        borderRadius: 9,
        background:
          "rgba(10,28,36,.32)",
      }}
    >
      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          justifyContent: "space-between",
        }}
      >
        <span
          style={{
            fontSize: 12,
            fontWeight: 700,
          }}
        >
          {name}
        </span>

        <BarChart3
          size={15}
          color="#66828e"
        />
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          marginTop: 16,
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "baseline",
          gap: 6,
        }}
      >
        <span
          style={{
            fontSize: 25,
            fontWeight: 700,
          }}
        >
          {vehicles}
        </span>

        <span
          className="muted"
          style={{
            fontSize: 9,
          }}
        >
          vehicles
        </span>
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          marginTop: 13,
          height: 6,
          borderRadius: 20,
          overflow: "hidden",
          background:
            "rgba(100,160,190,.10)",
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            width: `${percentage}%`,
            height: "100%",
            borderRadius: 20,
            background:
              percentage >= 70
                ? "#ef6a78"
                : percentage >= 40
                  ? "#e5b45b"
                  : "#31d29a",
          }}
        />
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          justifyContent:
            "space-between",
          marginTop: 9,
        }}
      >
        <span
          className="muted"
          style={{
            fontSize: 9,
          }}
        >
          {description}
        </span>

        <span
          style={{
            fontSize: 9,
            fontWeight: 700,
          }}
        >
          {percentage}%
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

  .report-button {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 7px;
    padding: 9px 13px;
    border: 1px solid rgba(20,190,235,.22);
    border-radius: 7px;
    background: rgba(20,190,235,.08);
    color: #9ddbe8;
    font-size: 10px;
    font-weight: 600;
    cursor: pointer;
  }
`;

if (
  typeof document !== "undefined" &&
  !document.getElementById(
    "metropolis-report-button-style",
  )
) {
  const style =
    document.createElement("style");

  style.id =
    "metropolis-report-button-style";

  style.textContent =
    reportButtonStyle;

  document.head.appendChild(
    style,
  );
}

import {
  Activity,

  Clock3,

  Play,

  Settings2,
  ShieldAlert,
  TrafficCone,

} from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";

type Scenario = {

  name: string;
  description: string;
  duration: string;
  vehicles: number;
  trafficLevel: string;
  impact: string;

const scenarios: Scenario[] = [
  {
    id: 1,
    name: "Normal City Flow",
    description:
      "Simulate normal vehicle movement across all major city zones.",
    duration: "10 min",
    vehicles: 8,
    trafficLevel: "Moderate",
    impact: "Low",
    icon: <CarFront size={18} />,
  },
  {
    id: 2,
    name: "Peak Hour Traffic",
    description:
      "Simulate increased traffic volume during morning or evening peak hours.",
    duration: "15 min",
    vehicles: 18,
    trafficLevel: "High",
    impact: "Medium",
    icon: <TrafficCone size={18} />,
  },
  {
    id: 3,
    name: "Major Road Incident",
    description:
      "Simulate a road incident causing congestion and reduced vehicle speed.",
    duration: "12 min",
    vehicles: 14,
    trafficLevel: "Critical",
    impact: "High",
    icon: <ShieldAlert size={18} />,
  },
  {
    id: 4,
    name: "Emergency Response",
    description:
      "Simulate emergency vehicle movement and operational response.",
    duration: "8 min",
    vehicles: 10,
    trafficLevel: "High",
    impact: "High",
    icon: <Zap size={18} />,
  },
  {
    id: 5,
    name: "Low Traffic Period",
    description:
      "Simulate low-density traffic conditions during off-peak hours.",
    duration: "10 min",
    vehicles: 5,
    trafficLevel: "Low",
    impact: "Low",
    icon: <Gauge size={18} />,
  },
];

export default function Scenarios() {
  const [selectedScenario, setSelectedScenario] =
    useState<Scenario | null>(null);

  const [runningScenario, setRunningScenario] =
    useState<number | null>(null);

  const [completedScenario, setCompletedScenario] =
    useState<number | null>(null);

  const handleRunScenario = (scenario: Scenario) => {
    setSelectedScenario(scenario);
    setRunningScenario(scenario.id);
    setCompletedScenario(null);

    setTimeout(() => {
      setRunningScenario(null);
      setCompletedScenario(scenario.id);
    }, 3000);
  };

  const handleReset = () => {
    setSelectedScenario(null);
    setRunningScenario(null);
    setCompletedScenario(null);
  };

  return (
    <div className="metropolis-page">
      {/* =================================================
          HEADER
      ================================================= */}

      <div className="page-header">
        <div>
          <h1 className="page-title">
            Traffic Scenarios
          </h1>

          <p className="page-subtitle">
            Simulate different urban traffic conditions
            and evaluate operational impact
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="live-pill">
          <span className="live-dot" />
          SIMULATION READY
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* =================================================
          TOP STATUS
      ================================================= */}

      <div className="grid-4">
        <ScenarioStatusCard
          icon={<Activity size={21} />}
          label="Scenario Engine"
          value={
            runningScenario
              ? "Running"
              : "Ready"
          }
          status={
            runningScenario
              ? "running"
              : "good"
          }
        />

        <ScenarioStatusCard
          icon={<CarFront size={21} />}
          label="Simulated Vehicles"
          value={
            selectedScenario
              ? selectedScenario.vehicles.toString()
              : "8"
          }
          status="good"
        />

        <ScenarioStatusCard
          icon={<Gauge size={21} />}
          label="Traffic Level"
          value={
            selectedScenario
              ? selectedScenario.trafficLevel
              : "Moderate"
          }
          status={
            selectedScenario?.trafficLevel ===
            "Critical"
              ? "danger"
              : selectedScenario?.trafficLevel ===
                  "High"
                ? "warning"
                : "good"
          }
        />

        <ScenarioStatusCard
          icon={<Clock3 size={21} />}
          label="Duration"
          value={
            selectedScenario
              ? selectedScenario.duration
              : "10 min"
          }
          status="good"
        />
      </div>

      {/* =================================================
          ACTIVE SCENARIO
      ================================================= */}

      {selectedScenario && (
        <section
          className="panel"
          style={{
            marginTop: 18,
          }}
        >
          <div className="panel-header">
            <div>
              <h3 className="panel-title">
                Active Scenario
              </h3>

              <p
                className="muted"
                style={{
                  marginTop: 5,
                  fontSize: 12,
                }}
              >
                Current digital twin simulation
              </p>
            </div>

            {runningScenario ? (
              <span className="status status-medium">
                RUNNING
              </span>
            ) : completedScenario ===
              selectedScenario.id ? (
              <span className="status status-low">
                COMPLETED
              </span>
            ) : (
              <span className="status status-low">
                READY
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          <div
            style={{
              padding: 18,
              display: "grid",
              gridTemplateColumns:
                "1fr auto",
              gap: 20,
              alignItems: "center",
            }}
          >
            <div>
              <div
                style={{
                  display: "flex",
                  alignItems: "center",
                  gap: 10,
                }}
              >
                <div
                  className="scenario-icon-large"
                >
                  {selectedScenario.icon}
                </div>

                <div>
                  <h2
                    style={{
                      margin: 0,
                      fontSize: 17,
                    }}
                  >
                    {selectedScenario.name}
                  </h2>

                  <p
                    className="muted"
                    style={{
                      marginTop: 6,
                      fontSize: 10,
                      lineHeight: 1.6,
                    }}
                  >
                    {selectedScenario.description}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              {runningScenario ===
                selectedScenario.id && (
                <div
                  style={{
                    marginTop: 18,
                  }}
                >
                  <div
                    style={{
                      display: "flex",
                      justifyContent:
                        "space-between",
                      marginBottom: 7,
                    }}
                  >
                    <span
                      className="muted"
                      style={{
                        fontSize: 9,
                      }}
                    >
                      Simulation progress
                    </span>

                    <span
                      className="muted"
                      style={{
                        fontSize: 9,
                      }}
                    >
                      Processing...
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <div
                    style={{
                      height: 7,
                      borderRadius: 20,
                      background:
                        "rgba(100,160,190,.10)",
                      overflow: "hidden",
                    }}
                  >
                    <div
                      className="scenario-progress"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleReset}
              className="settings-secondary-button"
            >
              <RotateCcw size={14} />
              Reset
            </button>
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      {/* =================================================
          SCENARIO LIST
      ================================================= */}

      <section
        className="panel"
        style={{
          marginTop: 18,
        }}
      >
        <div className="panel-header">
          <div>
            <h3 className="panel-title">
              Available Scenarios
            </h3>

            <p
              className="muted"
              style={{
                marginTop: 5,
                fontSize: 12,
              }}
            >
              Select a scenario to run inside the
              METROPOLIS digital twin
            </p>
          </div>

          <Settings2 size={18} />
        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            padding: 18,
            display: "grid",
            gridTemplateColumns:
              "repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr))",
            gap: 14,
          }}
        >
          {scenarios.map((scenario) => (
            <ScenarioCard
              key={scenario.id}
              scenario={scenario}
              running={
                runningScenario ===
                scenario.id
              }
              completed={
                completedScenario ===
                scenario.id
              }
              onRun={() =>
                handleRunScenario(
                  scenario,
                )
              }
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* =================================================
          SCENARIO IMPACT
      ================================================= */}

      <section
        className="panel"
        style={{
          marginTop: 18,
        }}
      >
        <div className="panel-header">
          <div>
            <h3 className="panel-title">
              Simulation Impact
            </h3>

            <p
              className="muted"
              style={{
                marginTop: 5,
                fontSize: 12,
              }}
            >
              Operational metrics affected by the
              selected scenario
            </p>
          </div>

          <TrendingUp size={18} />
        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            padding: 18,
            display: "grid",
            gridTemplateColumns:
              "repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr))",
            gap: 14,
          }}
        >
          <ImpactCard
            icon={<CarFront size={17} />}
            title="Vehicle Volume"
            value={
              selectedScenario
                ? `${selectedScenario.vehicles}`
                : "8"
            }
            description="Simulated vehicles"
          />

          <ImpactCard
            icon={<Gauge size={17} />}
            title="Traffic Pressure"
            value={
              selectedScenario
                ? selectedScenario.trafficLevel
                : "Moderate"
            }
            description="Estimated traffic condition"
          />

          <ImpactCard
            icon={<Users size={17} />}
            title="Operational Impact"
            value={
              selectedScenario
                ? selectedScenario.impact
                : "Low"
            }
            description="Expected city impact"
          />
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* =================================================
          FOOTER
      ================================================= */}

      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          gap: 8,
          marginTop: 14,
          paddingBottom: 20,
          color: "#64808d",
          fontSize: 10,
        }}
      >
        <Activity size={13} />

        METROPOLIS scenario engine digitally simulates
        traffic conditions without requiring physical
        IoT hardware.
      </div>

      {/* =================================================
          LOCAL STYLES
      ================================================= */}

/* =========================================================
   SCENARIO STATUS CARD
========================================================= */

function ScenarioStatusCard({
  icon,
  label,
  value,
  status,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  label: string;
  value: string;
  status:
    | "good"
    | "warning"
    | "danger"
    | "running";
}) {
  const statusClass =
    status === "good"
      ? "status-low"
      : status === "warning"
        ? "status-medium"
        : status === "danger"
          ? "status-high"
          : "status-medium";

  return (
    <div className="panel kpi-card">
      {icon}

      <div
        className="kpi-label"
        style={{
          marginTop: 14,
        }}
      >
        {label}
      </div>

      <div
        className="kpi-value"
        style={{
          fontSize: 18,
        }}
      >
        {value}
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          marginTop: 7,
        }}
      >
        <span
          className={`status ${statusClass}`}
        >
          {status === "running"
            ? "RUNNING"
            : status === "good"
              ? "READY"
              : status === "warning"
                ? "WATCH"
                : "ALERT"}
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   SCENARIO CARD
========================================================= */

function ScenarioCard({
  scenario,
  running,
  completed,
  onRun,
}: {
  scenario: Scenario;
  running: boolean;
  completed: boolean;
  onRun: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      className={`scenario-card ${
        running || completed
          ? "selected"
          : ""
      }`}
    >
      <div className="scenario-card-header">
        <div className="scenario-card-icon">
          {scenario.icon}
        </div>

        <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
          <div className="scenario-card-title">
            {scenario.name}
          </div>

          <div className="scenario-card-description">
            {scenario.description}
          </div>
        </div>

        {completed && (
          <CheckCircle2
            size={16}
            style={{
              color: "#31d29a",
              flexShrink: 0,
            }}
          />
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="scenario-meta">
        <div className="scenario-meta-item">
          <Clock3 size={11} />
          {scenario.duration}
        </div>

        <div className="scenario-meta-item">
          <CarFront size={11} />
          {scenario.vehicles} vehicles
        </div>

        <div className="scenario-meta-item">
          <Gauge size={11} />
          {scenario.trafficLevel}
        </div>

        <div className="scenario-meta-item">
          <ShieldAlert size={11} />
          {scenario.impact} impact
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="scenario-actions">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onRun}
          disabled={running}
          className={`scenario-run-button ${
            running
              ? "running"
              : completed
                ? "completed"
                : ""
          }`}
        >
          {running ? (
            <>
              <Activity size={13} />
              Running...
            </>
          ) : completed ? (
            <>
              <CheckCircle2 size={13} />
              Completed
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <Play size={13} />
              Run Scenario
            </>
          )}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   IMPACT CARD
========================================================= */

function ImpactCard({
  icon,
  title,
  value,
  description,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  title: string;
  value: string;
  description: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        padding: 16,
        border:
          "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.09)",
        borderRadius: 9,
        background:
          "rgba(10,28,36,.32)",
      }}
    >
      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          gap: 9,
          color: "#7898a4",
        }}
      >
        {icon}

        <span
          style={{
            fontSize: 10,
            textTransform: "uppercase",
            letterSpacing: ".05em",
          }}
        >
          {title}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          marginTop: 13,
          fontSize: 18,
          fontWeight: 700,
        }}
      >
        {value}
      </div>

      <div
        className="muted"
        style={{
          marginTop: 7,
          fontSize: 9,
        }}
      >
        {description}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

              icon={<RefreshCw size={17} />}
              title="Vehicle Simulation"
              description="Continuously update simulated vehicles"
              right={
                <Toggle
                  checked={simulation}
                  onChange={setSimulation}
                />
              }
            />

            <SettingRow
              icon={<SlidersHorizontal size={17} />}
              title="Simulation Speed"
              description="Movement and environment update interval"

                      "1px solid rgba(80,170,205,.14)",

                  1x Normal

              title="Map Animation"

              right={

                />

              icon={<Globe size={17} />}
              title="Environment"
              description="Current simulation environment"
              right={
                <span

                    fontSize: 12,

                >
                  Digital Twin

                <span className="status status-low">
                  Secure

              METROPOLIS application environment
            </p>
          </div>

          <Database size={18} />
        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            display: "grid",

              "repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr))",
            gap: 10,

          <InfoBox
            label="Application"
            value="METROPOLIS"
          />

          <InfoBox

          />
