/* =========================================================
   SETTING ROW
   ========================================================= */

function SettingRow({
  icon,
  title,
  description,
  right,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  right: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: "flex",
        alignItems: "center",
        gap: 12,
        padding: "14px 0",
        borderBottom:
          "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.07)",
      }}
    >
      <div
        style={{
          width: 34,
          height: 34,
          flexShrink: 0,
          borderRadius: 8,
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          justifyContent: "center",
          background: "rgba(20,190,235,.06)",
        }}
      >
        {icon}
      </div>

      <div style={{ flex: 1 }}>
        <div
          style={{
            fontSize: 12,
            fontWeight: 600,
          }}
        >
          {title}
        </div>

        <div
          className="muted"
          style={{
            marginTop: 4,
            fontSize: 10,
          }}
        >
          {description}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div>{right}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   TOGGLE
   ========================================================= */

function Toggle({
  checked,
  onChange,
}: {
  checked: boolean;
  onChange: (value: boolean) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={() => onChange(!checked)}
      aria-label="Toggle setting"
      style={{
        width: 40,
        height: 22,
        padding: 2,
        borderRadius: 20,
        border: "none",
        cursor: "pointer",
        background: checked
          ? "rgba(24,201,239,.45)"
          : "rgba(100,130,145,.22)",
        display: "flex",
        alignItems: "center",
        justifyContent: checked
          ? "flex-end"
          : "flex-start",
      }}
    >
      <span
        style={{
          width: 18,
          height: 18,
          borderRadius: "50%",
          background: checked
            ? "#67dff5"
            : "#71848d",
          display: "block",
        }}
      />
    </button>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   INFORMATION BOX
   ========================================================= */

function InfoBox({
  label,
  value,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        padding: 14,
        borderRadius: 8,
        background: "rgba(5,20,32,.42)",
        border:
          "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.08)",
      }}
    >
      <div
        className="muted"
        style={{
          fontSize: 10,
          textTransform: "uppercase",
          letterSpacing: ".06em",
        }}
      >
        {label}
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          marginTop: 7,
          fontSize: 12,
          fontWeight: 600,
        }}
      >
        {value}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


========== FILE: src\pages\Traffic.tsx ==========
import {
  Activity,
  CarFront,
  CircleGauge,
  MapPin,
  Radio,
  TrendingUp,
  TriangleAlert,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useMemo } from "react";

import { useVehicleSimulation } from "../hooks/useVehicleSimulation";

export default function Traffic() {
  const { vehicles } = useVehicleSimulation();

  const movingVehicles = useMemo(
    () => vehicles.filter((vehicle) => vehicle.status === "Moving"),
    [vehicles],
  );

  const stoppedVehicles = useMemo(
    () => vehicles.filter((vehicle) => vehicle.status === "Stopped"),
    [vehicles],
  );

  const averageSpeed = useMemo(() => {
    if (vehicles.length === 0) {
      return 0;
    }

    return Math.round(
      vehicles.reduce((total, vehicle) => total + vehicle.speed, 0) /
        vehicles.length,
    );
  }, [vehicles]);

  const zoneData = useMemo(() => {
    const zones: Record<
      string,
      {
        vehicles: number;
        moving: number;
        stopped: number;
        averageSpeed: number;
      }
    > = {};

    vehicles.forEach((vehicle) => {
      if (!zones[vehicle.zone]) {
        zones[vehicle.zone] = {
          vehicles: 0,
          moving: 0,
          stopped: 0,
          averageSpeed: 0,
        };
      }

      zones[vehicle.zone].vehicles += 1;

      if (vehicle.status === "Moving") {
        zones[vehicle.zone].moving += 1;
      }

      if (vehicle.status === "Stopped") {
        zones[vehicle.zone].stopped += 1;
      }

      zones[vehicle.zone].averageSpeed += vehicle.speed;
    });

    Object.keys(zones).forEach((zone) => {
      if (zones[zone].vehicles > 0) {
        zones[zone].averageSpeed = Math.round(
          zones[zone].averageSpeed / zones[zone].vehicles,
        );
      }
    });

    return zones;
  }, [vehicles]);

  const trafficLevel = useMemo(() => {
    if (vehicles.length === 0) {
      return "Low";
    }

    const stoppedPercentage =
      (stoppedVehicles.length / vehicles.length) * 100;

    if (stoppedPercentage >= 40) {
      return "High";
    }

    if (stoppedPercentage >= 20) {
      return "Moderate";
    }

    return "Low";
  }, [vehicles.length, stoppedVehicles.length]);

  const getTrafficClass = (level: string) => {
    if (level === "High") {
      return "status status-high";
    }

    if (level === "Moderate") {
      return "status status-medium";
    }

    return "status status-low";
  };

  return (
    <div className="metropolis-page">
      {/* HEADER */}

      <div className="page-header">
        <div>
          <h1 className="page-title">Traffic Intelligence</h1>

          <p className="page-subtitle">
            Real-time simulated traffic monitoring and congestion analysis
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="live-pill">
          <span className="live-dot" />
          TRAFFIC LIVE
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* KPI CARDS */}

      <div className="grid-4">
        <TrafficCard
          icon={<CarFront size={21} />}
          label="Total Vehicles"
          value={vehicles.length.toString()}
          description="Currently simulated"
        />

        <TrafficCard
          icon={<Radio size={21} />}
          label="Moving Vehicles"
          value={movingVehicles.length.toString()}
          description="Vehicles in motion"
        />

        <TrafficCard
          icon={<CircleGauge size={21} />}
          label="Average Speed"
          value={`${averageSpeed} km/h`}
          description="Across all vehicles"
        />

        <TrafficCard
          icon={<TriangleAlert size={21} />}
          label="Traffic Level"
          value={trafficLevel}
          description="Current simulation status"
        />
      </div>

      {/* TRAFFIC OVERVIEW */}

      <div
        className="grid-2"
        style={{
          marginTop: 18,
          alignItems: "start",
        }}
      >
        {/* CURRENT TRAFFIC STATUS */}

        <section className="panel">
          <div className="panel-header">
            <div>
              <h3 className="panel-title">Current Traffic Status</h3>

              <p
                className="muted"
                style={{
                  marginTop: 5,
                  fontSize: 12,
                }}
              >
                Simulated city-wide traffic conditions
              </p>
            </div>

            <Activity size={18} />
          </div>

          <div style={{ padding: 18 }}>
            <TrafficStatusRow
              icon={<TrendingUp size={17} />}
              title="Overall Traffic"
              value={trafficLevel}
              className={getTrafficClass(trafficLevel)}
            />

            <TrafficStatusRow
              icon={<CarFront size={17} />}
              title="Moving Vehicles"
              value={`${movingVehicles.length} vehicles`}
              className="status status-low"
            />

            <TrafficStatusRow
              icon={<TriangleAlert size={17} />}
              title="Stopped Vehicles"
              value={`${stoppedVehicles.length} vehicles`}
              className={
                stoppedVehicles.length > 0
                  ? "status status-medium"
                  : "status status-low"
              }
            />

            <TrafficStatusRow
              icon={<CircleGauge size={17} />}
              title="Average Speed"
              value={`${averageSpeed} km/h`}
              className="status status-low"
            />

            <TrafficStatusRow
              icon={<Radio size={17} />}
              title="Simulation Engine"
              value="Active"
              className="status status-low"
            />
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* SPEED MONITOR */}

        <section className="panel">
          <div className="panel-header">
            <div>
              <h3 className="panel-title">Speed Monitoring</h3>

              <p
                className="muted"
                style={{
                  marginTop: 5,
                  fontSize: 12,
                }}
              >
                Current vehicle speed distribution
              </p>
            </div>

            <CircleGauge size={18} />
          </div>

          <div style={{ padding: 18 }}>
            <SpeedBar
              label="Low Speed"
              value={
                vehicles.filter((vehicle) => vehicle.speed < 20).length
              }
              total={vehicles.length}
            />

            <SpeedBar
              label="Normal Speed"
              value={
                vehicles.filter(
                  (vehicle) => vehicle.speed >= 20 && vehicle.speed < 50,
                ).length
              }
              total={vehicles.length}
            />

            <SpeedBar
              label="High Speed"
              value={
                vehicles.filter((vehicle) => vehicle.speed >= 50).length
              }
              total={vehicles.length}
            />
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>

      {/* ZONE TRAFFIC */}

      <section
        className="panel"
        style={{ marginTop: 18 }}
      >
        <div className="panel-header">
          <div>
            <h3 className="panel-title">Traffic by Zone</h3>

            <p
              className="muted"
              style={{
                marginTop: 5,
                fontSize: 12,
              }}
            >
              Vehicle activity across simulated zones
            </p>
          </div>

          <MapPin size={18} />
        </div>

        <div style={{ padding: 18 }}>
          {Object.keys(zoneData).length === 0 ? (
            <div
              className="muted"
              style={{
                padding: 25,
                textAlign: "center",
              }}
            >
              No vehicle data available.
            </div>
          ) : (
            Object.entries(zoneData).map(([zone, data]) => {
              const percentage =
                vehicles.length > 0
                  ? Math.round((data.vehicles / vehicles.length) * 100)
                  : 0;

              const zoneTraffic =
                data.stopped > data.moving
                  ? "High"
                  : data.stopped > 0
                    ? "Moderate"
                    : "Low";

              return (
                <div
                  key={zone}
                  style={{
                    marginBottom: 22,
                  }}
                >
                  <div
                    style={{
                      display: "flex",
                      justifyContent: "space-between",
                      alignItems: "center",
                      marginBottom: 8,
                    }}
                  >
                    <div>
                      <div
                        style={{
                          fontSize: 13,
                          fontWeight: 600,
                        }}
                      >
                        {zone}
                      </div>

                      <div
                        className="muted"
                        style={{
                          fontSize: 10,
                          marginTop: 3,
                        }}
                      >
                        {data.moving} moving · {data.stopped} stopped ·{" "}
                        {data.averageSpeed} km/h
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div
                      style={{
                        display: "flex",
                        alignItems: "center",
                        gap: 10,
                      }}
                    >
                      <span className={getTrafficClass(zoneTraffic)}>
                        {zoneTraffic}
                      </span>

                      <span
                        className="muted"
                        style={{
                          fontSize: 11,
                        }}
                      >
                        {data.vehicles} vehicles
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div
                    style={{
                      height: 8,
                      borderRadius: 20,
                      background: "rgba(100,160,190,.10)",
                      overflow: "hidden",
                    }}
                  >
                    <div
                      style={{
                        width: `${percentage}%`,
                        height: "100%",
                        borderRadius: 20,
                        background:
                          zoneTraffic === "High"
                            ? "#ef6a78"
                            : zoneTraffic === "Moderate"
                              ? "#e5b45b"
                              : "#31d29a",
                        transition: "width .3s ease",
                      }}
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })
          )}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* LIVE TRAFFIC TABLE */}

      <section
        className="panel"
        style={{ marginTop: 18 }}
      >
        <div className="panel-header">
          <div>
            <h3 className="panel-title">Live Traffic Monitor</h3>

            <p
              className="muted"
              style={{
                marginTop: 5,
                fontSize: 12,
              }}
            >
              Vehicle-level traffic information
            </p>
          </div>

          <span className="status status-low">LIVE</span>
        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            overflowX: "auto",
          }}
        >
          <table
            style={{
              width: "100%",
              borderCollapse: "collapse",
              minWidth: 760,
            }}
          >
            <thead>
              <tr
                style={{
                  color: "#688493",
                  fontSize: 10,
                  textTransform: "uppercase",
                  letterSpacing: ".06em",
                }}
              >
                <th style={tableHeader}>Vehicle</th>
                <th style={tableHeader}>Road</th>
                <th style={tableHeader}>Zone</th>
                <th style={tableHeader}>Speed</th>
                <th style={tableHeader}>Status</th>
                <th style={tableHeader}>Traffic</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>

            <tbody>
              {vehicles.slice(0, 10).map((vehicle) => {
                const vehicleTraffic =
                  vehicle.status === "Stopped"
                    ? "High"
                    : vehicle.speed < 20
                      ? "Moderate"
                      : "Low";

                return (
                  <tr key={vehicle.id}>
                    <td style={tableCell}>
                      <strong>{vehicle.id}</strong>
                    </td>

                    <td style={tableCell}>{vehicle.road}</td>

                    <td style={tableCell}>{vehicle.zone}</td>

                    <td style={tableCell}>{vehicle.speed} km/h</td>

                    <td style={tableCell}>
                      <span
                        className={
                          vehicle.status === "Moving"
                            ? "status status-low"
                            : "status status-medium"
                        }
                      >
                        {vehicle.status}
                      </span>
                    </td>

                    <td style={tableCell}>
                      <span className={getTrafficClass(vehicleTraffic)}>
                        {vehicleTraffic}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* FOOTER STATUS */}

      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          gap: 8,
          marginTop: 14,
          color: "#64808d",
          fontSize: 10,
        }}
      >
        <Activity size={13} />

        METROPOLIS traffic engine is continuously analyzing simulated vehicle
        movement and congestion.
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          marginTop: 18,
          paddingBottom: 20,
          color: "#506b78",
          fontSize: 10,
        }}
      >
        Total vehicles: {vehicles.length} · Moving:{" "}
        {movingVehicles.length} · Stopped: {stoppedVehicles.length} · Mode:
        Digital Twin
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   TRAFFIC CARD
========================================================= */

function TrafficCard({
  icon,
  label,
  value,
  description,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  label: string;
  value: string;
  description: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="panel kpi-card">
      {icon}

      <div
        className="kpi-label"
        style={{
          marginTop: 14,
        }}
      >
        {label}
      </div>

      <div className="kpi-value">{value}</div>

      <div className="kpi-change">{description}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   TRAFFIC STATUS ROW
========================================================= */

function TrafficStatusRow({
  icon,
  title,
  value,
  className,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  title: string;
  value: string;
  className: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: "flex",
        alignItems: "center",
        gap: 12,
        padding: "13px 0",
        borderBottom: "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.07)",
      }}
    >
      <div
        style={{
          width: 32,
          height: 32,
          borderRadius: 7,
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          justifyContent: "center",
          background: "rgba(20,190,235,.06)",
        }}
      >
        {icon}
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          flex: 1,
          fontSize: 12,
          fontWeight: 600,
        }}
      >
        {title}
      </div>

      <span className={className}>{value}</span>
    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   SPEED BAR
========================================================= */

function SpeedBar({
  label,
  value,
  total,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: number;
  total: number;
}) {
  const percentage =
    total > 0 ? Math.round((value / total) * 100) : 0;

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        marginBottom: 20,
      }}
    >
      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          justifyContent: "space-between",
          marginBottom: 7,
        }}
      >
        <span
          style={{
            fontSize: 12,
            fontWeight: 600,
          }}
        >
          {label}
        </span>

        <span
          className="muted"
          style={{
            fontSize: 11,
          }}
        >
          {value} vehicles · {percentage}%
        </span>
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          height: 7,
          borderRadius: 20,
          background: "rgba(100,160,190,.10)",
          overflow: "hidden",
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            width: `${percentage}%`,
            height: "100%",
            borderRadius: 20,
            background:
              label === "High Speed"
                ? "#ef6a78"
                : label === "Normal Speed"
                  ? "#31d29a"
                  : "#e5b45b",
            transition: "width .3s ease",
          }}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

   TABLE STYLES
========================================================= */

  textAlign: "left",
  padding: "13px 16px",
  borderBottom: "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.08)",
};

const tableCell: React.CSSProperties = {
  padding: "14px 16px",
  borderBottom: "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.06)",
  fontSize: 11,
};


========== FILE: src\pages\VehicleDetails.tsx ==========
import {
  Activity,
  ArrowLeft,
  CarFront,
  CircleGauge,
  Compass,
  MapPin,
  Navigation,
  Radio,
  Route,
  Signal,
  Timer,
  Database,
  Gauge,
} from "lucide-react";

import { useEffect, useMemo } from "react";
import { useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";

import { useVehicleSimulation } from "../hooks/useVehicleSimulation";

export default function VehicleDetails() {
  const { vehicleId } = useParams<{
    vehicleId: string;
  }>();

  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const {
    vehicles,
    loading,
    error,
    selectVehicle,
    clearVehicleSelection,
  } = useVehicleSimulation();

  /* =========================================================
     FIND SELECTED VEHICLE
  ========================================================= */

  const vehicle = useMemo(() => {
    if (!vehicleId) {
      return undefined;
    }

    return vehicles.find(
      (item) =>
        String(item.id).toLowerCase() ===
        String(vehicleId).toLowerCase(),
    );
  }, [vehicles, vehicleId]);

  /* =========================================================
     SELECT VEHICLE
  ========================================================= */

  useEffect(() => {
    if (vehicleId) {
      selectVehicle(vehicleId);
    }

    return () => {
      clearVehicleSelection();
    };
  }, [
    vehicleId,
    selectVehicle,
    clearVehicleSelection,
  ]);

  /* =========================================================
     LOADING
  ========================================================= */

  if (loading && !vehicle) {
    return (
      <div className="metropolis-page">
        <div className="page-header">
          <div>
            <h1 className="page-title">
              Vehicle Details
            </h1>

            <p className="page-subtitle">
              Loading vehicle information from the
              METROPOLIS vehicle intelligence engine...
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="live-pill">
            <span className="live-dot" />
            VEHICLE INTELLIGENCE
          </div>
        </div>

        <section className="panel">
          <div
            style={{
              padding: 60,
              textAlign: "center",
            }}
          >
            <Activity
              size={40}
              style={{
                opacity: 0.55,
                marginBottom: 14,
                animation: "pulse 1.5s infinite",
              }}
            />

            <h2
              style={{
                fontSize: 18,
                marginBottom: 8,
              }}
            >
              Loading vehicle
            </h2>

            <p
              className="muted"
              style={{
                fontSize: 12,
              }}
            >
              Fetching live simulated vehicle data...
            </p>
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
    );
  }

  /* =========================================================
     VEHICLE NOT FOUND
  ========================================================= */

  if (!vehicle) {
    return (
      <div className="metropolis-page">
        <div className="page-header">
          <div>
            <h1 className="page-title">
              Vehicle Not Found
            </h1>

            <p className="page-subtitle">
              The requested vehicle could not be
              found in the METROPOLIS digital twin.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="live-pill">
            <span className="live-dot" />
            VEHICLE INTELLIGENCE
          </div>
        </div>

        <section className="panel">
          <div
            style={{
              padding: 50,
              textAlign: "center",
            }}
          >
            <CarFront
              size={42}
              style={{
                marginBottom: 14,
                opacity: 0.45,
              }}
            />

            <h2
              style={{
                fontSize: 18,
                marginBottom: 8,
              }}
            >
              Vehicle unavailable
            </h2>

            <p
              className="muted"
              style={{
                fontSize: 12,
                marginBottom: 8,
              }}
            >
              Vehicle ID{" "}
              <strong>
                {vehicleId || "N/A"}
              </strong>{" "}
              is not currently available.
            </p>

            {error && (
              <p
                style={{
                  color: "#e5b45b",
                  fontSize: 11,
                  marginBottom: 20,
                }}
              >
                {error}
              </p>
            )}

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => navigate("/vehicles")}
              style={backButton}
            >
              <ArrowLeft size={15} />
              Back to Vehicles
            </button>
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
    );
  }

  /* =========================================================
     STATUS
  ========================================================= */

  const isMoving =
    vehicle.status?.toLowerCase() ===
    "moving";

  const statusClass = isMoving
    ? "status-low"
    : "status-medium";

  /* =========================================================
     VEHICLE TYPE
  ========================================================= */

  const vehicleType =
    String(vehicle.type || "car")
      .charAt(0)
      .toUpperCase() +
    String(vehicle.type || "car").slice(1);

  /* =========================================================
     LOCATION
  ========================================================= */

  const latitude = Number(
    vehicle.latitude,
  ).toFixed(6);

  const longitude = Number(
    vehicle.longitude,
  ).toFixed(6);

  /* =========================================================
     TRAFFIC LEVEL
  ========================================================= */

  const trafficLevel =
    vehicle.status?.toLowerCase() ===
    "stopped"
      ? "High"
      : vehicle.speed < 20
        ? "Moderate"
        : "Low";

  const trafficClass =
    trafficLevel === "High"
      ? "status-high"
      : trafficLevel === "Moderate"
        ? "status-medium"
        : "status-low";

  /* =========================================================
     SPEED BAND
  ========================================================= */

  const speedBand =
    vehicle.speed < 20
      ? "Low"
      : vehicle.speed < 50
        ? "Normal"
        : "High";

  const speedBandClass =
    speedBand === "High"
      ? "status-high"
      : speedBand === "Normal"
        ? "status-low"
        : "status-medium";

  /* =========================================================
     MAIN PAGE
  ========================================================= */

  return (
    <div className="metropolis-page">

      {/* =====================================================
          HEADER
      ===================================================== */}

      <div className="page-header">
        <div>

          <div
            style={{
              display: "flex",
              alignItems: "center",
              gap: 10,
              marginBottom: 10,
            }}
          >
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() =>
                navigate("/vehicles")
              }
              style={iconBackButton}
              aria-label="Back to vehicles"
            >
              <ArrowLeft size={16} />
            </button>

            <span
              className="muted"
              style={{
                fontSize: 10,
                textTransform: "uppercase",
                letterSpacing: ".08em",
              }}
            >
              Vehicle Registry
            </span>
          </div>

          <h1 className="page-title">
            {vehicle.id}
          </h1>

          <p className="page-subtitle">
            Detailed live information for the
            selected simulated vehicle
          </p>

        </div>

        <div className="live-pill">
          <span className="live-dot" />
          LIVE VEHICLE
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* =====================================================
          VEHICLE IDENTITY
      ===================================================== */}

      <section
        className="panel"
        style={{
          marginBottom: 18,
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            padding: 20,
            display: "flex",
            alignItems: "center",
            justifyContent: "space-between",
            gap: 20,
            flexWrap: "wrap",
          }}
        >

          {/* VEHICLE BASIC INFO */}

          <div
            style={{
              display: "flex",
              alignItems: "center",
              gap: 15,
            }}
          >

            <div
              style={{
                width: 58,
                height: 58,
                borderRadius: 12,
                display: "flex",
                alignItems: "center",
                justifyContent: "center",
                background:
                  "rgba(20,190,235,.08)",
                border:
                  "1px solid rgba(20,190,235,.10)",
              }}
            >
              <CarFront size={27} />
            </div>

            <div>

              <div
                className="muted"
                style={{
                  fontSize: 9,
                  textTransform: "uppercase",
                  letterSpacing: ".08em",
                }}
              >
                Vehicle ID
              </div>

              <h2
                style={{
                  marginTop: 4,
                  fontSize: 20,
                  fontWeight: 750,
                }}
              >
                {vehicle.id}
              </h2>

              <div
                className="muted"
                style={{
                  marginTop: 4,
                  fontSize: 11,
                }}
              >
                {vehicle.model ||
                  "Simulated Vehicle"}
              </div>

            </div>
          </div>

          {/* STATUS */}

          <div
            style={{
              display: "flex",
              alignItems: "center",
              gap: 10,
              flexWrap: "wrap",
            }}
          >

            <span
              className={`status ${statusClass}`}
            >
              {vehicle.status}
            </span>

            <span
              className={`status ${trafficClass}`}
            >
              Traffic: {trafficLevel}
            </span>

            <span className="status status-medium">
              {vehicleType}
            </span>

            <span className="status status-medium">
              {vehicle.zone}
            </span>

          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* =====================================================
          KPI CARDS
      ===================================================== */}

      <div className="grid-4">

        <VehicleMetric
          icon={
            <CircleGauge size={21} />
          }
          label="Current Speed"
          value={`${vehicle.speed} km/h`}
          description={
            isMoving
              ? "Vehicle in motion"
              : "Vehicle stopped"
          }
        />

        <VehicleMetric
          icon={
            <Compass size={21} />
          }
          label="Direction"
          value={
            vehicle.direction ||
            "N/A"
          }
          description="Current travel direction"
        />

        <VehicleMetric
          icon={
            <Route size={21} />
          }
          label="Road"
          value={
            vehicle.road ||
            "N/A"
          }
          description="Current road segment"
        />

        <VehicleMetric
          icon={
            <MapPin size={21} />
          }
          label="Zone"
          value={
            vehicle.zone ||
            "N/A"
          }
          description="Current monitored zone"
        />

      </div>

      {/* =====================================================
          MAIN INFORMATION
      ===================================================== */}

      <div
        className="grid-2"
        style={{
          marginTop: 18,
          alignItems: "start",
        }}
      >

        {/* ===================================================
            VEHICLE INFORMATION
        =================================================== */}

        <section className="panel">

          <div className="panel-header">

            <div>
              <h3 className="panel-title">
                Vehicle Information
              </h3>

              <p
                className="muted"
                style={{
                  marginTop: 5,
                  fontSize: 12,
                }}
              >
                Registered simulated vehicle
                information
              </p>
            </div>

            <CarFront size={18} />

          </div>

          <div
            style={{
              padding: 18,
            }}
          >

            <DetailRow
              icon={
                <CarFront size={15} />
              }
              label="Vehicle ID"
              value={vehicle.id}
            />

            <DetailRow
              icon={
                <Radio size={15} />
              }
              label="Vehicle Type"
              value={vehicleType}
            />

            <DetailRow
              icon={
                <CarFront size={15} />
              }
              label="Model"
              value={
                vehicle.model ||
                "Simulated Vehicle"
              }
            />

            <DetailRow
              icon={
                <Signal size={15} />
              }
              label="Plate Number"
              value={
                vehicle.plate ||
                "N/A"
              }
              monospace
            />

            <DetailRow
              icon={
                <Navigation size={15} />
              }
              label="Direction"
              value={
                vehicle.direction ||
                "N/A"
              }
            />

            <DetailRow
              icon={
                <Activity size={15} />
              }
              label="Status"
              value={
                vehicle.status ||
                "Unknown"
              }
              status
              statusClass={
                statusClass
              }
            />

          </div>
        </section>

        {/* ===================================================
            LIVE LOCATION
        =================================================== */}

        <section className="panel">

          <div className="panel-header">

            <div>
              <h3 className="panel-title">
                Live Location
              </h3>

              <p
                className="muted"
                style={{
                  marginTop: 5,
                  fontSize: 12,
                }}
              >
                Current digital twin position
              </p>
            </div>

            <MapPin size={18} />

          </div>

          <div
            style={{
              padding: 18,
            }}
          >

            <DetailRow
              icon={
                <Route size={15} />
              }
              label="Road"
              value={
                vehicle.road ||
                "Main Road"
              }
            />

            <DetailRow
              icon={
                <MapPin size={15} />
              }
              label="Zone"
              value={
                vehicle.zone ||
                "Zone A"
              }
            />

            <DetailRow
              icon={
                <Navigation size={15} />
              }
              label="Latitude"
              value={latitude}
              monospace
            />

            <DetailRow
              icon={
                <Navigation size={15} />
              }
              label="Longitude"
              value={longitude}
              monospace
            />

            <DetailRow
              icon={
                <Timer size={15} />
              }
              label="Last Updated"
              value={
                vehicle.lastUpdated ||
                "Just now"
              }
            />

          </div>
        </section>

      </div>

      {/* =====================================================
          LIVE TELEMETRY
      ===================================================== */}

      <section
        className="panel"
        style={{
          marginTop: 18,
        }}
      >

        <div className="panel-header">

          <div>
            <h3 className="panel-title">
              Live Telemetry
            </h3>

            <p
              className="muted"
              style={{
                marginTop: 5,
                fontSize: 12,
              }}
            >
              Continuously updated simulated
              vehicle telemetry
            </p>
          </div>

          <Activity size={18} />

        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            padding: 18,
            display: "grid",
            gridTemplateColumns:
              "repeat(4, 1fr)",
            gap: 12,
          }}
        >

          <TelemetryCard
            label="Speed"
            value={String(
              vehicle.speed,
            )}
            unit="km/h"
            icon={
              <CircleGauge size={16} />
            }
          />

          <TelemetryCard
            label="Latitude"
            value={latitude}
            unit=""
            icon={
              <MapPin size={16} />
            }
          />

          <TelemetryCard
            label="Longitude"
            value={longitude}
            unit=""
            icon={
              <MapPin size={16} />
            }
          />

          <TelemetryCard
            label="Route Index"
            value={String(
              vehicle.routeIndex,
            )}
            unit=""
            icon={
              <Route size={16} />
            }
          />

        </div>
      </section>

      {/* =====================================================
          VEHICLE TRAFFIC ANALYSIS
      ===================================================== */}

      <section
        className="panel"
        style={{
          marginTop: 18,
        }}
      >

        <div className="panel-header">

          <div>
            <h3 className="panel-title">
              Vehicle Traffic Analysis
            </h3>

            <p
              className="muted"
              style={{
                marginTop: 5,
                fontSize: 12,
              }}
            >
              Current traffic interpretation for
              this vehicle
            </p>
          </div>

          <Gauge size={18} />

        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            padding: 18,
            display: "grid",
            gridTemplateColumns:
              "repeat(3, 1fr)",
            gap: 12,
          }}
        >

          <AnalysisCard
            label="Traffic Level"
            value={trafficLevel}
            className={trafficClass}
          />

          <AnalysisCard
            label="Movement"
            value={
              isMoving
                ? "Active"
                : "Stopped"
            }
            className={
              isMoving
                ? "status-low"
                : "status-medium"
            }
          />

          <AnalysisCard
            label="Speed Band"
            value={speedBand}
            className={
              speedBandClass
            }
          />

        </div>
      </section>

      {/* =====================================================
          DATA SOURCE
      ===================================================== */}

      <section
        className="panel"
        style={{
          marginTop: 18,
        }}
      >

        <div className="panel-header">

          <div>
            <h3 className="panel-title">
              Vehicle Data Source
            </h3>

            <p
              className="muted"
              style={{
                marginTop: 5,
                fontSize: 12,
              }}
            >
              Current METROPOLIS data pipeline
            </p>
          </div>

          <Database size={18} />

        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            padding: 18,
          }}
        >

          <SystemRow
            icon={
              <Database size={16} />
            }
            title="Vehicle Data Stream"
            value={
              error
                ? "Fallback"
                : "Connected"
            }
          />

          <SystemRow
            icon={
              <Navigation size={16} />
            }
            title="Position Engine"
            value="Active"
          />

          <SystemRow
            icon={
              <Activity size={16} />
            }
            title="Simulation Engine"
            value="Running"
          />

          <SystemRow
            icon={
              <Radio size={16} />
            }
            title="Tracking Status"
            value="Live"
          />

        </div>
      </section>

      {/* =====================================================
          FOOTER
      ===================================================== */}

      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          gap: 8,
          marginTop: 14,
          paddingBottom: 20,
          color: "#64808d",
          fontSize: 10,
        }}
      >
        <Activity size={13} />

        METROPOLIS vehicle intelligence engine
        continuously updates the selected
        vehicle's simulated position and status.
      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   VEHICLE METRIC
========================================================= */

function VehicleMetric({
  icon,
  label,
  value,
  description,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  label: string;
  value: string;
  description: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="panel kpi-card">

      {icon}

      <div
        className="kpi-label"
        style={{
          marginTop: 14,
        }}
      >
        {label}
      </div>

      <div
        className="kpi-value"
        style={{
          fontSize:
            value.length > 16
              ? 17
              : undefined,
        }}
      >
        {value}
      </div>

      <div className="kpi-change">
        {description}
      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   DETAIL ROW
========================================================= */

function DetailRow({
  icon,
  label,
  value,
  monospace = false,
  status = false,
  statusClass = "status-low",
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  label: string;
  value: string;
  monospace?: boolean;
  status?: boolean;
  statusClass?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: "flex",
        alignItems: "center",
        gap: 12,
        padding: "13px 0",
        borderBottom:
          "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.07)",
      }}
    >

      <div
        style={{
          width: 32,
          height: 32,
          borderRadius: 7,
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          justifyContent: "center",
          background:
            "rgba(100,160,190,.045)",
          color: "#78929e",
          flexShrink: 0,
        }}
      >
        {icon}
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          flex: 1,
        }}
      >

        <div
          className="muted"
          style={{
            fontSize: 9,
          }}
        >
          {label}
        </div>

        {status ? (
          <div
            style={{
              marginTop: 5,
            }}
          >
            <span
              className={`status ${statusClass}`}
            >
              {value}
            </span>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div
            style={{
              marginTop: 4,
              fontSize: 11,
              fontWeight: 650,
              fontFamily:
                monospace
                  ? "monospace"
                  : undefined,
            }}
          >
            {value}
          </div>
        )}

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   TELEMETRY CARD
========================================================= */

function TelemetryCard({
  icon,
  label,
  value,
  unit,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  label: string;
  value: string;
  unit: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        padding: 14,
        borderRadius: 8,
        background:
          "rgba(100,160,190,.035)",
        border:
          "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.07)",
      }}
    >

      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          gap: 7,
          color: "#64808d",
          fontSize: 9,
          textTransform: "uppercase",
          letterSpacing: ".06em",
        }}
      >
        {icon}
        {label}
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          marginTop: 9,
          fontSize: 16,
          fontWeight: 750,
        }}
      >
        {value}

        {unit && (
          <span
            className="muted"
            style={{
              fontSize: 9,
              marginLeft: 5,
              fontWeight: 500,
            }}
          >
            {unit}
          </span>
        )}
      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   ANALYSIS CARD
========================================================= */

function AnalysisCard({
  label,
  value,
  className,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  className: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        padding: 16,
        borderRadius: 9,
        background:
          "rgba(100,160,190,.035)",
        border:
          "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.07)",
      }}
    >

      <div
        className="muted"
        style={{
          fontSize: 9,
          textTransform: "uppercase",
          letterSpacing: ".06em",
        }}
      >
        {label}
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          marginTop: 9,
        }}
      >
        <span
          className={`status ${className}`}
        >
          {value}
        </span>
      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   SYSTEM ROW
========================================================= */

function SystemRow({
  icon,
  title,
  value,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  title: string;
  value: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: "flex",
        alignItems: "center",
        gap: 12,
        padding: "12px 0",
        borderBottom:
          "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.07)",
      }}
    >

      <div
        style={{
          width: 32,
          height: 32,
          borderRadius: 7,
          display: "flex",
          alignItems: "center",
          justifyContent: "center",
          background:
            "rgba(49,210,154,.07)",
        }}
      >
        {icon}
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          flex: 1,
          fontSize: 11,
          fontWeight: 600,
        }}
      >
        {title}
      </div>

      <span className="status status-low">
        {value}
      </span>

    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   BUTTON STYLES
========================================================= */

const backButton: React.CSSProperties = {
  display: "inline-flex",
  alignItems: "center",
  justifyContent: "center",
  gap: 8,
  border:
    "1px solid rgba(20,190,235,.20)",
  background:
    "rgba(20,190,235,.07)",
  color: "#d8f8ff",
  borderRadius: 7,
  padding: "9px 15px",
  fontSize: 10,
  fontWeight: 650,
  cursor: "pointer",
};

const iconBackButton: React.CSSProperties = {
  width: 32,
  height: 32,
  display: "inline-flex",
  alignItems: "center",
  justifyContent: "center",
  border:
    "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.10)",
  background:
    "rgba(100,160,190,.035)",
  color: "#9ab4be",
  borderRadius: 7,
  cursor: "pointer",
};


========== FILE: src\pages\Vehicles.tsx ==========
import {
  Activity,
  CarFront,
  CircleGauge,
  MapPin,
  Navigation,
  Radio,
  Search,
  Signal,
  Square,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

import { useVehicleSimulation } from "../hooks/useVehicleSimulation";

export default function Vehicles() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const { vehicles } =
    useVehicleSimulation();

  const [search, setSearch] =
    useState("");

  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] =
    useState<
      "All" | "Moving" | "Stopped"
    >("All");

  /* =========================================================
     FILTERED VEHICLES
  ========================================================= */

  const filteredVehicles = useMemo(() => {
    const query =
      search
        .trim()
        .toLowerCase();

    return vehicles.filter(
      (vehicle) => {
        const matchesSearch =
          query === "" ||
          vehicle.id
            .toLowerCase()
            .includes(query) ||
          vehicle.plate
            .toLowerCase()
            .includes(query) ||
          vehicle.type
            .toLowerCase()
            .includes(query) ||
          vehicle.road
            .toLowerCase()
            .includes(query) ||
          vehicle.zone
            .toLowerCase()
            .includes(query);

        const matchesStatus =
          statusFilter === "All" ||
          vehicle.status ===
            statusFilter;

        return (
          matchesSearch &&
          matchesStatus
        );
      },
    );
  }, [
    vehicles,
    search,
    statusFilter,
  ]);

  /* =========================================================
     FLEET STATISTICS
  ========================================================= */

  const movingVehicles =
    vehicles.filter(
      (vehicle) =>
        vehicle.status ===
        "Moving",
    ).length;

  const stoppedVehicles =
    vehicles.filter(
      (vehicle) =>
        vehicle.status ===
        "Stopped",
    ).length;

  const averageSpeed =
    vehicles.length === 0
      ? 0
      : Math.round(
          vehicles.reduce(
            (
              total,
              vehicle,
            ) =>
              total +
              vehicle.speed,
            0,
          ) /
            vehicles.length,
        );

  /* =========================================================
     OPEN VEHICLE DETAILS
  ========================================================= */

  const openVehicleDetails = (
    vehicleId: string,
  ) => {
    navigate(
      `/vehicles/${encodeURIComponent(
        vehicleId,
      )}`,
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="metropolis-page">
      {/* =====================================================
          HEADER
      ===================================================== */}

      <div className="page-header">
        <div>
          <h1 className="page-title">
            Vehicle Intelligence
          </h1>

          <p className="page-subtitle">
            Real-time simulated vehicle
            tracking and information
            management
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="live-pill">
          <span className="live-dot" />

          LIVE TRACKING
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* =====================================================
          KPI CARDS
      ===================================================== */}

      <div className="grid-4">
        <VehicleCard
          icon={
            <CarFront size={21} />
          }
          label="Total Vehicles"
          value={vehicles.length.toString()}
          description="Currently simulated"
        />

        <VehicleCard
          icon={
            <Navigation
              size={21}
            />
          }
          label="Moving"
          value={movingVehicles.toString()}
          description="Vehicles in motion"
        />

        <VehicleCard
          icon={
            <Square size={21} />
          }
          label="Stopped"
          value={stoppedVehicles.toString()}
          description="Currently stopped"
        />

        <VehicleCard
          icon={
            <CircleGauge
              size={21}
            />
          }
          label="Average Speed"
          value={`${averageSpeed} km/h`}
          description="Current fleet average"
        />
      </div>

      {/* =====================================================
          SEARCH + FILTER
      ===================================================== */}

      <section
        className="panel"
        style={{
          marginTop: 18,
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            padding: 16,
            display: "flex",
            alignItems: "center",
            gap: 12,
            flexWrap: "wrap",
          }}
        >
          {/* SEARCH */}

          <div
            style={{
              position:
                "relative",
              flex: 1,
              minWidth: 240,
            }}
          >
            <Search
              size={16}
              style={{
                position:
                  "absolute",
                left: 13,
                top: "50%",
                transform:
                  "translateY(-50%)",
                color: "#64808d",
              }}
            />

            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Search vehicle ID, plate, road, zone..."
              value={search}
              onChange={(event) =>
                setSearch(
                  event.target
                    .value,
                )
              }
              style={
                searchInput
              }
            />
          </div>

          {/* FILTER BUTTONS */}

          <div
            style={{
              display:
                "flex",
              gap: 7,
              flexWrap:
                "wrap",
            }}
          >
            <FilterButton
              active={
                statusFilter ===
                "All"
              }
              onClick={() =>
                setStatusFilter(
                  "All",
                )
              }
            >
              All
            </FilterButton>

            <FilterButton
              active={
                statusFilter ===
                "Moving"
              }
              onClick={() =>
                setStatusFilter(
                  "Moving",
                )
              }
            >
              Moving
            </FilterButton>

            <FilterButton
              active={
                statusFilter ===
                "Stopped"
              }
              onClick={() =>
                setStatusFilter(
                  "Stopped",
                )
              }
            >
              Stopped
            </FilterButton>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* =====================================================
          VEHICLE TABLE
      ===================================================== */}

      <section
        className="panel"
        style={{
          marginTop: 18,
        }}
      >
        <div className="panel-header">
          <div>
            <h3 className="panel-title">
              Live Vehicle Registry
            </h3>

            <p
              className="muted"
              style={{
                marginTop: 5,
                fontSize: 12,
              }}
            >
              {
                filteredVehicles.length
              }{" "}
              vehicles currently
              matching the selected
              filters
            </p>
          </div>

          <div
            style={{
              display:
                "flex",
              alignItems:
                "center",
              gap: 7,
            }}
          >
            <Signal size={15} />

            <span className="status status-low">
              CONNECTED
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            overflowX:
              "auto",
          }}
        >
          <table
            style={{
              width: "100%",
              minWidth: 1050,
              borderCollapse:
                "collapse",
            }}
          >
            <thead>
              <tr
                style={{
                  color:
                    "#688493",
                  fontSize: 10,
                  textTransform:
                    "uppercase",
                  letterSpacing:
                    ".06em",
                }}
              >
                <th
                  style={
                    tableHeader
                  }
                >
                  Vehicle
                </th>

                <th
                  style={
                    tableHeader
                  }
                >
                  Plate
                </th>

                <th
                  style={
                    tableHeader
                  }
                >
                  Type
                </th>

                <th
                  style={
                    tableHeader
                  }
                >
                  Location
                </th>

                <th
                  style={
                    tableHeader
                  }
                >
                  Road
                </th>

                <th
                  style={
                    tableHeader
                  }
                >
                  Zone
                </th>

                <th
                  style={
                    tableHeader
                  }
                >
                  Speed
                </th>

                <th
                  style={
                    tableHeader
                  }
                >
                  Direction
                </th>

                <th
                  style={
                    tableHeader
                  }
                >
                  Status
                </th>
              </tr>
            </thead>

            <tbody>
              {filteredVehicles.length >
              0 ? (
                filteredVehicles.map(
                  (
                    vehicle,
                  ) => (
                    <tr
                      key={
                        vehicle.id
                      }
                      onClick={() =>
                        openVehicleDetails(
                          vehicle.id,
                        )
                      }
                      style={{
                        cursor:
                          "pointer",
                        transition:
                          "background .2s ease",
                      }}
                      title={`Open details for ${vehicle.id}`}
                    >
                      {/* ===================================
                          VEHICLE
                      =================================== */}

                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        <div
                          style={{
                            display:
                              "flex",
                            alignItems:
                              "center",
                            gap: 10,
                          }}
                        >
                          <div
                            style={{
                              width: 34,
                              height: 34,
                              borderRadius: 8,
                              display:
                                "flex",
                              alignItems:
                                "center",
                              justifyContent:
                                "center",
                              background:
                                "rgba(20,190,235,.07)",
                            }}
                          >
                            <CarFront
                              size={
                                16
                              }
                            />
                          </div>

                          <div>
                            <strong
                              style={{
                                fontSize: 11,
                              }}
                            >
                              {
                                vehicle.id
                              }
                            </strong>

                            <div
                              className="muted"
                              style={{
                                fontSize: 8,
                                marginTop: 3,
                              }}
                            >
                              VIEW
                              DETAILS
                              →
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </td>

                      {/* ===================================
                          PLATE
                      =================================== */}

                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        <span
                          style={{
                            fontFamily:
                              "monospace",
                            fontSize: 11,
                            fontWeight:
                              600,
                          }}
                        >
                          {
                            vehicle.plate
                          }
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      {/* ===================================
                          TYPE
                      =================================== */}

                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        {
                          vehicle.type
                        }
                      </td>

                      {/* ===================================
                          LOCATION
                      =================================== */}

                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        <div
                          style={{
                            display:
                              "flex",
                            alignItems:
                              "center",
                            gap: 5,
                          }}
                        >
                          <MapPin
                            size={
                              13
                            }
                          />

                          <span>
                            {formatLocation(
                              vehicle,
                            )}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      </td>

                      {/* ===================================
                          ROAD
                      =================================== */}

                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        {
                          vehicle.road
                        }
                      </td>

                      {/* ===================================
                          ZONE
                      =================================== */}

                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        <span className="status status-medium">
                          {
                            vehicle.zone
                          }
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      {/* ===================================
                          SPEED
                      =================================== */}

                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        <strong>
                          {
                            vehicle.speed
                          }
                        </strong>{" "}
                        km/h
                      </td>

                      {/* ===================================
                          DIRECTION
                      =================================== */}

                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        {
                          vehicle.direction ||
                          "N/A"
                        }
                      </td>

                      {/* ===================================
                          STATUS
                      =================================== */}

                      <td
                        style={
                          tableCell
                        }
                      >
                        <span
                          className={
                            vehicle.status ===
                            "Moving"
                              ? "status status-low"
                              : "status status-medium"
                          }
                        >
                          {
                            vehicle.status
                          }
                        </span>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ),
                )
              ) : (
                <tr>
                  <td
                    colSpan={
                      9
                    }
                    style={{
                      padding:
                        "45px 20px",
                      textAlign:
                        "center",
                      color:
                        "#64808d",
                      fontSize: 12,
                    }}
                  >
                    No vehicles
                    found
                    matching
                    the
                    selected
                    filters.
                  </td>
                </tr>
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* =====================================================
          VEHICLE ACTIVITY SUMMARY
      ===================================================== */}

      <div
        className="grid-2"
        style={{
          marginTop: 18,
          alignItems:
            "start",
        }}
      >
        {/* ================================================
            LIVE ENGINE
        ================================================= */}

        <section className="panel">
          <div className="panel-header">
            <div>
              <h3 className="panel-title">
                Vehicle Simulation
                Engine
              </h3>

              <p
                className="muted"
                style={{
                  marginTop: 5,
                  fontSize: 12,
                }}
              >
                Current digital twin
                engine status
              </p>
            </div>

            <Activity size={18} />
          </div>

          <div
            style={{
              padding: 18,
            }}
          >
            <InfoRow
              icon={
                <Radio
                  size={16}
                />
              }
              title="Simulation Engine"
              value="Running"
              good
            />

            <InfoRow
              icon={
                <Signal
                  size={16}
                />
              }
              title="Vehicle Data Stream"
              value="Connected"
              good
            />

            <InfoRow
              icon={
                <Navigation
                  size={16}
                />
              }
              title="Position Updates"
              value="Active"
              good
            />

            <InfoRow
              icon={
                <CarFront
                  size={16}
                />
              }
              title="Tracked Vehicles"
              value={vehicles.length.toString()}
              good
            />
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* ================================================
            FLEET SUMMARY
        ================================================= */}

        <section className="panel">
          <div className="panel-header">
            <div>
              <h3 className="panel-title">
                Fleet Summary
              </h3>

              <p
                className="muted"
                style={{
                  marginTop: 5,
                  fontSize: 12,
                }}
              >
                Current vehicle
                distribution
              </p>
            </div>

            <CarFront size={18} />
          </div>

          <div
            style={{
              padding: 18,
            }}
          >
            <SummaryItem
              label="Total Fleet"
              value={
                vehicles.length
              }
            />

            <SummaryItem
              label="Moving Vehicles"
              value={
                movingVehicles
              }
            />

            <SummaryItem
              label="Stopped Vehicles"
              value={
                stoppedVehicles
              }
            />

            <SummaryItem
              label="Average Speed"
              value={`${averageSpeed} km/h`}
            />
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>

      {/* =====================================================
          FOOTER
      ===================================================== */}

      <div
        style={{
          display:
            "flex",
          alignItems:
            "center",
          gap: 8,
          marginTop: 14,
          paddingBottom: 20,
          color:
            "#64808d",
          fontSize: 10,
        }}
      >
        <Activity size={13} />

        METROPOLIS vehicle
        intelligence engine
        continuously
        updates simulated
        vehicle positions
        and status.
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   VEHICLE CARD
========================================================= */

function VehicleCard({
  icon,
  label,
  value,
  description,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  label: string;
  value: string;
  description: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="panel kpi-card">
      {icon}

      <div
        className="kpi-label"
        style={{
          marginTop: 14,
        }}
      >
        {label}
      </div>

      <div className="kpi-value">
        {value}
      </div>

      <div className="kpi-change">
        {description}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   FILTER BUTTON
========================================================= */

function FilterButton({
  active,
  children,
  onClick,
}: {
  active: boolean;
  children: React.ReactNode;
  onClick: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClick}
      style={{
        border: active
          ? "1px solid rgba(20,190,235,.35)"
          : "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.10)",
        background: active
          ? "rgba(20,190,235,.09)"
          : "rgba(100,160,190,.035)",
        color: active
          ? "#d8f8ff"
          : "#78929e",
        borderRadius: 7,
        padding:
          "8px 14px",
        fontSize: 10,
        fontWeight: 600,
        cursor:
          "pointer",
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   INFO ROW
========================================================= */

function InfoRow({
  icon,
  title,
  value,
  good,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  title: string;
  value: string;
  good: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display:
          "flex",
        alignItems:
          "center",
        gap: 12,
        padding:
          "12px 0",
        borderBottom:
          "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.07)",
      }}
    >
      <div
        style={{
          width: 32,
          height: 32,
          borderRadius: 7,
          display:
            "flex",
          alignItems:
            "center",
          justifyContent:
            "center",
          background:
            good
              ? "rgba(49,210,154,.07)"
              : "rgba(239,106,120,.07)",
        }}
      >
        {icon}
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          flex: 1,
          fontSize: 11,
          fontWeight: 600,
        }}
      >
        {title}
      </div>

      <span
        className={
          good
            ? "status status-low"
            : "status status-medium"
        }
      >
        {value}
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   SUMMARY ITEM
========================================================= */

function SummaryItem({
  label,
  value,
}: {
  label: string;
  value:
    | string
    | number;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display:
          "flex",
        justifyContent:
          "space-between",
        alignItems:
          "center",
        padding:
          "12px 0",
        borderBottom:
          "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.07)",
      }}
    >
      <span
        className="muted"
        style={{
          fontSize: 10,
        }}
      >
        {label}
      </span>

      <strong
        style={{
          fontSize: 12,
        }}
      >
        {value}
      </strong>
    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   LOCATION FORMATTER
========================================================= */

function formatLocation(
  vehicle: any,
) {
  if (
    vehicle.location &&
    typeof vehicle.location ===
      "string"
  ) {
    return vehicle.location;
  }

  if (
    vehicle.latitude !==
      undefined &&
    vehicle.longitude !==
      undefined
  ) {
    return `${Number(
      vehicle.latitude,
    ).toFixed(
      4,
    )}, ${Number(
      vehicle.longitude,
    ).toFixed(
      4,
    )}`;
  }

  return "Digital Twin";
}
