   TABLE STYLES
========================================================= */

const searchInput: React.CSSProperties =
  {
    width: "100%",
    height: 38,
    boxSizing:
      "border-box",
    border:
      "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.10)",
    outline: "none",
    borderRadius: 7,
    background:
      "rgba(100,160,190,.035)",
    color: "#d8edf3",
    padding:
      "0 12px 0 38px",
    fontSize: 11,
  };

const tableHeader: React.CSSProperties =
  {
    textAlign:
      "left",
    padding:
      "13px 16px",
    borderBottom:
      "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.08)",
  };

const tableCell: React.CSSProperties =
  {
    padding:
      "14px 16px",
    borderBottom:
      "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.06)",
    fontSize: 11,
  };


========== FILE: src\pages\Zones.tsx ==========
import {
  Activity,
  AlertTriangle,
  CarFront,
  CircleGauge,
  MapPin,
  Navigation,
  Radio,
  ShieldCheck,
  TrendingUp,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useMemo } from "react";

import { useVehicleSimulation } from "../hooks/useVehicleSimulation";

interface ZoneData {
  name: string;
  description: string;
  capacity: number;
  trafficLevel: "Low" | "Medium" | "High";
  baseCongestion: number;
}

const zones: ZoneData[] = [
  {
    name: "Zone A",
    description: "Central Business District",
    capacity: 120,
    trafficLevel: "High",
    baseCongestion: 78,
  },
  {
    name: "Zone B",
    description: "Residential & Commercial Area",
    capacity: 100,
    trafficLevel: "Medium",
    baseCongestion: 54,
  },
  {
    name: "Zone C",
    description: "Airport & Industrial Corridor",
    capacity: 80,
    trafficLevel: "Low",
    baseCongestion: 32,
  },
];

export default function Zones() {
  const { vehicles } = useVehicleSimulation();

  const zoneStats = useMemo(() => {
    return zones.map((zone) => {
      const zoneVehicles = vehicles.filter(
        (vehicle) => vehicle.zone === zone.name,
      );

      const moving = zoneVehicles.filter(
        (vehicle) => vehicle.status === "Moving",
      ).length;

      const stopped = zoneVehicles.filter(
        (vehicle) => vehicle.status === "Stopped",
      ).length;

      const averageSpeed =
        zoneVehicles.length === 0
          ? 0
          : Math.round(
              zoneVehicles.reduce(
                (total, vehicle) =>
                  total + vehicle.speed,
                0,
              ) / zoneVehicles.length,
            );

      const occupancy = Math.min(
        100,
        Math.round(
          (zoneVehicles.length /
            zone.capacity) *
            100,
        ),
      );

      const congestion = Math.min(
        100,
        Math.max(
          zone.baseCongestion +
            Math.round(
              (zoneVehicles.length - 10) *
                1.5,
            ),
          5,
        ),
      );

      return {
        ...zone,
        vehicleCount: zoneVehicles.length,
        moving,
        stopped,
        averageSpeed,
        occupancy,
        congestion,
      };
    });
  }, [vehicles]);

  const totalVehicles = vehicles.length;

  const totalMoving = vehicles.filter(
    (vehicle) => vehicle.status === "Moving",
  ).length;

  const totalStopped = vehicles.filter(
    (vehicle) => vehicle.status === "Stopped",
  ).length;

  const averageSpeed =
    vehicles.length === 0
      ? 0
      : Math.round(
          vehicles.reduce(
            (total, vehicle) =>
              total + vehicle.speed,
            0,
          ) / vehicles.length,
        );

  const highestTrafficZone =
    [...zoneStats].sort(
      (a, b) =>
        b.congestion -
        a.congestion,
    )[0];

  return (
    <div className="metropolis-page">
      {/* =========================================
          HEADER
      ========================================= */}

      <div className="page-header">
        <div>
          <h1 className="page-title">
            Zone Intelligence
          </h1>

          <p className="page-subtitle">
            Monitor traffic, occupancy and vehicle
            activity across simulated city zones
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="live-pill">
          <span className="live-dot" />
          ZONE MONITOR
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* =========================================
          KPI CARDS
      ========================================= */}

      <div className="grid-4">
        <ZoneCard
          icon={<MapPin size={21} />}
          label="Active Zones"
          value={zones.length.toString()}
          description="Digitally monitored"
        />

        <ZoneCard
          icon={<CarFront size={21} />}
          label="Total Vehicles"
          value={totalVehicles.toString()}
          description={`${totalMoving} currently moving`}
        />

        <ZoneCard
          icon={<CircleGauge size={21} />}
          label="Average Speed"
          value={`${averageSpeed} km/h`}
          description="Across all zones"
        />

        <ZoneCard
          icon={<AlertTriangle size={21} />}
          label="Highest Traffic"
          value={
            highestTrafficZone?.name ||
            "N/A"
          }
          description={
            highestTrafficZone
              ? `${highestTrafficZone.congestion}% congestion`
              : "No data"
          }
        />
      </div>

      {/* =========================================
          ZONE CARDS
      ========================================= */}

      <div
        className="grid-3"
        style={{
          marginTop: 18,
          alignItems: "stretch",
        }}
      >
        {zoneStats.map(
          (zone) => (
            <ZonePanel
              key={zone.name}
              zone={zone}
            />

      {/* =========================================

      ========================================= */}

      <section

          marginTop: 18,

      >
        <div className="panel-header">

              Zone Operations
            </h3>

            <p
              className="muted"
              style={{
                marginTop: 5,
                fontSize: 12,
              }}

              Real-time simulated activity by
              monitored zone
            </p>
          </div>

          <Activity size={18} />
        </div>

        <div
          style={{
            overflowX: "auto",
          }}
        >
          <table
            style={{
              width: "100%",
              minWidth: 850,
              borderCollapse:
                "collapse",
            }}
          >
            <thead>
              <tr
                style={{
                  color: "#688493",
                  fontSize: 10,

                    "uppercase",
                  letterSpacing:
                    ".06em",
                }}
              >
                <th style={tableHeader}>

                <th style={tableHeader}>
                  Vehicles
                </th>

                <th style={tableHeader}>
                  Moving
                </th>

                  Stopped

                  Avg Speed
                </th>

                  Occupancy
                </th>

                  Congestion
                </th>

                  Status
                </th>
              </tr>

                    key={zone.name}

                      <div
                        style={{

                            "flex",

                        </div>

                        {
                          zone.vehicleCount
                        }
                      </strong>
                    </td>

                    <td

            style={{
              padding: 18,
            }}
          >
            {zoneStats.map(
              (zone) => {
                const percentage =
                  totalVehicles ===
                  0
                    ? 0
                    : Math.round(
                        (zone.vehicleCount /
                          totalVehicles) *
                          100,
                      );

                return (
                  <div
                    key={
                      zone.name
                    }
                    style={{
                      marginBottom:
                        18,
                    }}

                    <div
                      style={{

                          "flex",

                          7,

                        {
                          zone.name
                        }
                      </span>

                        className="muted"
                        style={{
                          fontSize: 10,
                        }}
                      >
                        {
                          zone.vehicleCount
                        }{" "}
                        vehicles ·{" "}
                        {
                          percentage
                        }
                        %
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <ProgressBar
                      percentage={
                        percentage
                      }
                    />
                  </div>
                );
              },
            )}
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="panel">

/* =========================================================
   STATUS ROW
========================================================= */

function StatusRow({
  icon,
  title,
  value,
  good,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  title: string;
  value: string;
  good: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display:
          "flex",
        alignItems:
          "center",
        gap: 12,
        padding:
          "13px 0",
        borderBottom:
          "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.07)",
      }}
    >
      <div
        style={{
          width: 32,
          height: 32,
          borderRadius: 7,
          display:
            "flex",
          alignItems:
            "center",
          justifyContent:
            "center",
          background:
            good
              ? "rgba(49,210,154,.07)"
              : "rgba(239,106,120,.07)",
        }}
      >
        {icon}
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          flex: 1,
          fontSize: 11,
          fontWeight: 600,
        }}
      >
        {title}
      </div>

      <span
        className={`status ${
          good
            ? "status-low"
            : "status-medium"
        }`}
      >
        {value}
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   PROGRESS BAR
========================================================= */

function ProgressBar({
  percentage,
}: {
  percentage: number;
}) {
  const safePercentage =
    Math.min(
      100,
      Math.max(
        0,
        percentage,
      ),
    );

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        height: 6,
        borderRadius: 20,
        overflow: "hidden",
        background:
          "rgba(100,160,190,.10)",
      }}
    >
      <div
        style={{
          width: `${safePercentage}%`,
          height: "100%",
          borderRadius: 20,
          background:
            safePercentage >= 70
              ? "#ef6a78"
              : safePercentage >= 40
                ? "#e5b45b"
                : "#31d29a",
          transition:
            "width .3s ease",
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

/* =========================================================
   HELPERS
========================================================= */

function getTrafficStatus(
  congestion: number,
) {
  if (congestion >= 75) {
    return "High";
  }

  if (congestion >= 45) {
    return "Moderate";
  }

  return "Low";
}

function getTrafficStatusClass(
  congestion: number,
) {
  if (congestion >= 75) {
    return "status-high";
  }

  if (congestion >= 45) {
    return "status-medium";
  }

  return "status-low";
}

};

const tableCell: React.CSSProperties = {
  padding:
    "14px 16px",
  borderBottom:
    "1px solid rgba(100,160,190,.06)",
  fontSize: 11,
};

========== FILE: src\services\api.js ==========
const API_BASE_URL = "http://localhost:5000/api";

// ======================================================
// COMMON API REQUEST FUNCTION
// ======================================================

const apiRequest = async (endpoint, options = {}) => {
    try {
        const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}${endpoint}`, {
            headers: {
                "Content-Type": "application/json",
                ...(options.headers || {})
            },
            ...options
        });

        const data = await response.json();

        if (!response.ok) {
            throw new Error(
                data.message || `API request failed: ${response.status}`
            );
        }

        return data;
    } catch (error) {
        console.error(`API Error [${endpoint}]:`, error);
        throw error;
    }
};

// ======================================================
// HEALTH
// ======================================================

export const getHealth = () => {
    return apiRequest("/health");
};

// ======================================================
// VEHICLES
// ======================================================

export const getVehicles = () => {
    return apiRequest("/vehicles");
};

export const getVehicleById = (id) => {
    return apiRequest(`/vehicles/${id}`);
};

// TRAFFIC
// ======================================================

export const getTraffic = () => {
    return apiRequest("/traffic");
};

// INCIDENTS
// ======================================================

export const getIncidents = () => {
    return apiRequest("/incidents");
};

export const getIncidentById = (id) => {
    return apiRequest(`/incidents/${id}`);
};

// ======================================================
// ZONES
// ======================================================

export const getZones = () => {
    return apiRequest("/zones");
};

export const getZoneById = (id) => {
    return apiRequest(`/zones/${id}`);
};

// ANALYTICS

};

const api = {
    getHealth,
    getVehicles,
    getVehicleById,
    getTraffic,
    getIncidents,
    getIncidentById,
    getZones,
    getZoneById,
    getAnalytics
};

export default api;


========== FILE: src\services\api.ts ==========
const API_BASE_URL = "http://localhost:5000/api";

/* =========================================================
   TYPES
========================================================= */

export interface ApiResponse<T> {
  success: boolean;
  message?: string;
  data?: T;
  error?: string;
}

/* =========================================================
   VEHICLE
========================================================= */

export interface Vehicle {
  /* Frontend fields */

  id: string;

  plate?: string;

  type?: string;

  model?: string;

  road?: string;

  zone?: string;

  speed: number;

  direction?: string;

  status: string;

  latitude?: number;

  longitude?: number;

  position?: [
    number,
    number,
  ];

  lastUpdated?: string;

  /* Backend-compatible fields */

  vehicle_id?: string;

  plate_number?: string;

  number_plate?: string;

  vehicle_type?: string;

  road_name?: string;

  zone_name?: string;

  lat?: number;

  lng?: number;

  last_updated?: string;

  vehicle_name?: string;

  name?: string;
}

/* =========================================================
   TRAFFIC
========================================================= */

export interface TrafficData {
  id?: number | string;

  zone?: string;

  level?: string;

  density?: number;

  averageSpeed?: number;

  vehicleCount?: number;

  status?: string;
}

   INCIDENT
========================================================= */

export interface Incident {
  id: number | string;

  type: string;

  description: string;

  location: string;

  severity: string;

  status: string;

  timestamp?: string;
}

/* =========================================================
   ZONE
========================================================= */

export interface Zone {
  id: number | string;

  name: string;

  status?: string;

  vehicleCount?: number;

  trafficLevel?: string;

  averageSpeed?: number;
}

/* =========================================================
   ANALYTICS
========================================================= */

export interface AnalyticsData {
  totalVehicles?: number;

  movingVehicles?: number;

  stoppedVehicles?: number;

  averageSpeed?: number;

  incidents?: number;

  congestion?: number;
}

/* =========================================================
   COMMON REQUEST FUNCTION
========================================================= */

async function apiRequest<T>(
  endpoint: string,
  options: RequestInit = {},
): Promise<T> {
  try {
    const response =
      await fetch(
        `${API_BASE_URL}${endpoint}`,
        {
          headers: {
            "Content-Type":
              "application/json",

            ...options.headers,
          },

          ...options,
        },
      );

    const contentType =
      response.headers.get(
        "content-type",
      );

    const result =
      contentType?.includes(
        "application/json",
      )
        ? await response.json()
        : await response.text();

    /* ---------------------------------------------
       API ERROR
    --------------------------------------------- */

    if (!response.ok) {
      const message =
        typeof result ===
          "object" &&
        result?.message
          ? result.message
          : `API request failed: ${response.status}`;

      throw new Error(
        message,
      );
    }

    return result as T;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      `API request failed: ${endpoint}`,
      error,
    );

    throw error;
  }
}

/* =========================================================
   HEALTH
========================================================= */

export async function checkBackendHealth() {
  return apiRequest<{
    success: boolean;

    message: string;

    database?: string;

    server?: string;
  }>("/health");
}

/* =========================================================
   VEHICLES
========================================================= */

/*
 * GET ALL VEHICLES
 *
 * Expected backend response:
 *
 * {
 *   success: true,
 *   data: [...]
 * }
 */

export async function getVehicles() {
  return apiRequest<{
    success: boolean;

    data: Vehicle[];

    message?: string;

    error?: string;
  }>("/vehicles");
}

/*
 * GET SINGLE VEHICLE
 *
 * Expected backend response:
 *
 * {
 *   success: true,
 *   data: {...}
 * }
 */

export async function getVehicle(
  id: string,
) {
  return apiRequest<{
    success: boolean;

    data: Vehicle;

    message?: string;

    error?: string;
  }>(
    `/vehicles/${encodeURIComponent(
      id,
    )}`,
  );
}

/*
 * CREATE VEHICLE
 */

export async function createVehicle(
  vehicle: Partial<Vehicle>,
) {
  return apiRequest<{
    success: boolean;

    data: Vehicle;

    message?: string;
  }>("/vehicles", {
    method: "POST",

    body: JSON.stringify(
      vehicle,
    ),
  });
}

/*
 * UPDATE VEHICLE
 */

export async function updateVehicle(
  id: string,
  vehicle: Partial<Vehicle>,
) {
  return apiRequest<{
    success: boolean;

    data: Vehicle;

    message?: string;
  }>(
    `/vehicles/${encodeURIComponent(
      id,
    )}`,
    {
      method: "PUT",

      body: JSON.stringify(
        vehicle,
      ),
    },
  );
}

/*
 * DELETE VEHICLE
 */

export async function deleteVehicle(
  id: string,
) {
  return apiRequest<{
    success: boolean;

    message: string;
  }>(
    `/vehicles/${encodeURIComponent(
      id,
    )}`,
    {
      method: "DELETE",
    },
  );
}

========================================================= */

  scenario: string,
) {

    success: boolean;
